# OMF Battlegrounds — Reverse Engineering Findings

Goal: native macOS reimplementation of the "DE" engine (Diversions Entertainment, 2003) that runs the original game data, ScummVM/OpenOMF style. Original binaries are reference only; they are never shipped or patched.

Evidence key: **[REA ev_…]** = REA evidence ID · **[ghidra]** = headless Ghidra 12.1.4 export in `re/dump/<module>/` · **[shell]** = plain `strings`/`xxd`/PE-header observation · *inference* = not directly observed.

## Artifacts

| File | SHA-256 / notes |
|---|---|
| `Input2.ModuleDLL` | `812945ee…6c786c`, PE x86 DLL, base 0x30000000 [REA ev_56c2af54…] |
| `OMFBG.exe` | SafeDisc 2 wrapped launcher (sections `stxt774`, `stxt371`) [shell]. Its code is not decrypted; only its readable symbol and import tables are used (FINDINGS 123). |
| `*.ModuleDLL` ×9 | Unprotected. Standard `.text/.rdata/.data/.reloc` sections [shell] |

## Module map (imports [shell])

| Module | Depends on | Platform APIs → macOS replacement |
|---|---|---|
| Core | dbghelp, kernel32, user32, gdi32, comdlg32, advapi32, shell32, ole32, rpcrt4, winmm, avifil32, ws2_32 | Object system, serialization, script VM, compression → portable C++ |
| Creature | Core | Creature/actor model |
| Session | Core, Creature | Game session / match rules |
| OMF | Core, Creature, Session | OMF-specific game classes (fighters, pilots, factions) |
| Direct3D | Core, Creature, **d3d9** | Renderer → Metal or OpenGL via SDL |
| Input2 | Core, **dinput8**, winmm | Input → SDL GameController/keyboard |
| AudioPlayer | Core, ole32 | Audio → SDL_mixer / OpenAL |
| Particles | Core | Particle systems |
| GameSpy | Core, wsock32 | Online lobby (GameSpy service is defunct) → stub |

## Findings ledger

1. **Exports carry full MSVC-mangled C++ names**, so class and method names are recovered. Engine classes use the prefix `DE_C`. [ghidra `re/dump/Input2/exports.tsv`]
2. **Input2** implements `DE_CDirectInputControllerComponent` (GetAxisState, GetButtonState, HasStateChanged, GetNextStateTime…) and is registered via `DE_CInitInputModule2Factory`. It has 325 functions and 32 classes. [ghidra]
3. **Object model:** `DE_CObject`, `DE_CObjectFactory`, `DE_CContents`, `DE_CReferenceOrigin/Target`, `DE_CGUID`, `DE_CRegistry`, `DE_CDataBlock`. Modules register factories into Core. [ghidra Input2 classes.txt]
4. **All game data is in 65 `.DEObjects` files (728 MB).** [shell]
   - Header: u32 length 0x27, then `"Diversions Entertainment Objects File2"`, then `00 02 00 00 00 00`.
   - Strings are u32-length-prefixed, length including the NUL.
   - Objects carry 16-byte GUIDs; the node suffix `…004033de174c` is common to them.
   - Objects carry type paths such as `TransferAgent\Win\File` and `UniqueActorProperty\Force\Common`.
5. **Payloads are compressed.** `Creature.DEObjects` contains an LZ-style stream with visible literal runs. [shell] Core has `DE_CLZRWContentsCompressor` (LZRW family) and `inflate 1.0 Copyright 1995-1996 Mark Adler` (zlib). [shell strings]
6. **The game logic is scripted.** The `Creature` "Health" Behavior's payload contains C-like source (`float m_…; // This is how much health the creature has`). Core contains the compiler and VM: `DE_CPCodeMethod`, `DE_CClassCompiledMethod`, `DE_CClassExecutorInstance::ExecuteInstant`, and messages such as "began compiling", "did not compile correctly therefore brain cannot run" and "…VMCode was saved…". [shell strings] *Inference:* most gameplay is script ("brains") loaded from data, so an engine reimplementation plus a script VM could run the original content.

7. **Container layout (verified on Creature.DEObjects by `engine/tests/de_tests.cpp`):** `string` signature (`…File` or `…File2`), then for File2 a `u8` format version (2). For version > 1 the master object's vtable+0xAC reads a field (4 zero bytes in shipped data). Then the header `block` and the body. [ghidra Core 0x70051bb0 `DE_CTransferAgentWinFile::LoadHeader` + test]
   - `string` is a `u32` length that includes the NUL, followed by the bytes. [ghidra Core 0x70019b80 `Extract(char**)`]
   - `block` is a `u32` length followed by raw bytes. [ghidra Core 0x70019c10 / 0x70019c90]
   - The header block opens with the `DE_CTransferAgent::ProcessHeader` version byte (9 in shipped data) and a squeezed size. Version 9 calls the master object's `ProcessContents` (vtable+0xA0), then the agent's own, then a count of child object-infos. [ghidra Core 0x70055a70]
   - *Inference:* every class level writes a nested chunk of version `u8`, squeezed size, then fields (seen as `04 41 …`, where 0x41 tracks the name length).
8. **Squeezed integer:** a byte below 0xFE is the value itself; 0xFE is followed by a `u16`, 0xFF by a `u32`. [ghidra Core 0x70019a60 `ExtractSqueezed`]
9. **`DE_CContents::Decompress` methods** (squeezed ID): 0 = stored `block`; 1–3 = `DE_CCompression` LZSS tree coder; 4 = unknown; 5 = rejected; 6 = squeezed size + LZRW3-A; 7 = 16-byte checksum + squeezed size + LZRW3-A. [ghidra Core 0x7001a3b0]
   - **The method-7 checksum is MD5 of the decoded bytes**, verified on Creature: `4dc5475a83156c70d14d398d093d6c1e`. [test]
10. **The LZRW3-A decoder is the standard algorithm:** 4096 slots in 8-way sets, START_STRING `"123456789012345678"`, FLAG_COPY = 1. The binary's hash multiplier (`0x…FE5F`) shares its low 13 bits with 40543, so the results are identical. [ghidra Core 0x7000c200 → `engine/src/de/lzrw3a.cpp`]
11. **Behaviors are stored as script source.** The Creature "Health" behavior decodes to sections `Import`, `Global` and `Members` (`float m_Health; // …`) plus methods such as `HealthChanged`, each with its source text. [test]
12. **Ghidra issue:** its "Non-Returning Functions – Discovered" analyzer wrongly flagged `DE_CDataBlock::DE_CDataBlock`, `operator delete`, `_gAllocateMemoryDebug` and others in Core. That truncated 4,729 call sites and left code undisassembled, including `DE_CTransferAgentWinFile::LoadHeader` (0x70051bb0), which references the file signature. Fix: `re/scripts/NoReturnOff.java` pre-script plus `re/scripts/reexport.sh`. [ghidra]

13. **The DLLs contain full COFF symbol tables.** Core has 49,689 entries, including 19,071 functions, which name static and internal functions too. [shell `re/proto/coffsyms.py`]
    - The symbol values are already RVAs. Ghidra 12.1.4 adds the section VA on top, so every COFF label lands `+0x1000` too far in `.text` (and by the section base in `.rdata`/`.data`).
    - Proof: for all 8,320 Core exports the export RVA equals the COFF value, and the same holds in the other 8 modules.
    - Example: the real `DE_CTransferAgent::ObjProcessContents` is at 0x700545B0, while Ghidra labelled 0x700555B0.
    - Fix: `re/scripts/FixCoffSymbols.java` pre-script, which moves labels and entry points using `re/coff/<module>.tsv`.
    - **Dumps made before this fix carry names shifted by one page for non-exported functions. Trust only post-fix dumps.**

14. **The header is a table of contents.** [ghidra Core 0x700545b0, 0x700557c0, 0x7004fdd0 + `engine/src/de/objects_file.cpp`]
    - After the agent chunk (v9) come the master object info, the agent's own chunk (v1: block, GUID, GUIDPool, BOOL), an `i32` count, then one object info per object.
    - Object info v4 holds: name `string`, class/factory path `string`, GUID, BOOL (1 byte), `i32`, `u32 flags`.
    - The WinFile extension (format 2, no version byte) is a size followed by a `u32` file offset of the object's data.
    - `ExtractBOOL` reads 1 byte [0x70019b00]. `DE_CGUIDPool` is a `u8` flag; if it's 1, a `u16` count and count×16 bytes follow [0x7001f360].
15. **Object data** at that offset is three `block`s (contents, references, hierarchy), then `ReadEXDataHeader`: a `u32` count, and per entry a `block` and a `u32`. [0x7004fbb0, 0x700511f0, vtable slot +0xAC resolved by `re/proto/vtable.py`]
    - `DE_CTransferObjectInfo::New` decompresses the contents **only when `flags & 2`** [0x70055140]. It then creates the object with `DE_CObjectFactoryManager::FindByPath(class_path)->NewObject()` and calls its `ObjProcessContents` (vtable +0xA0).
    - **All 65 files and 28,115 objects parse, and every compressed one passes its MD5 check** (`ctest`, test `de-all-files`).
16. **Class census:** 456 distinct class paths (`re/class-census.txt`).
    - The largest groups are TextureManager (3,327), `Image\Standard` (2,166), `DataChannel\UnitFloats` (2,152), Actor (2,070) and **`Noise2\DirectMusicSegment` (1,745)**, so audio is DirectMusic segments and needs a DirectMusic reimplementation.
    - Scripting objects: `Class` (255), `Behavior` (290), `ClassExecutor` (284).

17. **Class paths map to C++ classes.** Each factory's `PathAdd()` returns its path component and `ObjParent()` returns its parent factory, sometimes through the IAT for other modules. [ghidra `re/scripts/FactoryPaths.java` → `re/factory-paths.tsv`, `re/proto/factory_map.py`]
    - **All 28,115 objects resolve to one of 118 C++ classes** (`re/class-map.tsv`): 63 in Core, 20 Particles, 16 Creature, 15 OMF, 2 Session, 2 AudioPlayer.
    - The earlier "456 classes" census was an artefact: names containing spaces broke the whitespace split.
18. **References are a tag byte** [Core 0x70057690 `ExtractReference`]:
    - ≥ 0x32: local object, index = tag − 0x32. 1: `u16` local index.
    - 2, 3, 4: `u8`, `u16` or `u32` index into the agent GUID pool, meaning an object in another file.
    - Anything else: null.
    - Verified: `PFighterStats` refs `0x37` → Fighter object 5 = `BFighterStats`. [test]
19. **Behavior (`DE_CPCode`) v5 layout** [Core 0x70163d90 and element readers]: name, then the Import / Global / Members elements, then an `i32` count of (`i32` type 1=Method/2=Inclusion/3=Class + element chunk), then one `i32` parent link per element.
    - The links form a dotted method tree (`Ground.Attack.LeftPunchHeavy`).
    - **All 290 behaviors decode to original commented source, 5.4 MB in total** (`re/scripts-src/`).
    - The language is a C++/Java-like coroutine state language: `exEndFrame()` yields per frame; it has `master`, `inherit`, `replicate` and `final`.
20. **`Class` (`DE_CClass`)**: contents hold name, base factory path and a string pool of exported signatures. The references list the attached behaviors (BOOL-terminated). [Core 0x70135a90, 0x70135df0]
21. **Compiled bytecode is shipped.** EX key `VMC2` → payload of `u8` flag plus `block` [Core 0x70050130 `EXRequestData`]. 249 of the 255 Class objects have it. `DE_CClass::PrepareForExecution` loads it via `EXGet` and compiles only if it is missing [Core 0x7013abb0].
    - Container v13 [Core 0x70134530]:
      - change-info block
      - classes: `[name, sq layout-pos]`
      - intrinsics: `[sq class, signature]`
      - ref-members `[sq]`
      - per behavior a sized chunk [0x701370d0]: instances, compiled methods, base links, replicated vars
      - then globals and interfaces. Prototype: `re/proto/vmc2.py`.
    - A compiled method [0x7013de50] holds a `bool`, a name `block`, a **bytecode block**, 4 flags, conditional tables, instances, interfaces, 5 counters, the signature, an `i32` and replicated vars.
22. **Bytecode encoding** (interpreter `DE_CPCExecuter::ContinueExecutionInternal`, Core 0x7014eaa0, 15.6 KB, about 300 cases):
    - Each instruction is an `i32` opcode followed by typed operands (`ExtractBYTE/CHAR` = 1 byte, `WORD/SHORT` = 2, `DWORD/INT/FLOAT` = 4).
    - Opcodes 2 and 3 carry inline data of an operand-given length. Opcode 313 has a conditional `u16`. Opcode 310 has no case (no-op). Opcodes 109–112 are jumps (pc = code base + operand).
    - **All 8,279 compiled methods in the 223 dumped classes disassemble exactly to their end; 131 distinct opcodes are used.** [`re/proto/disasm.py`, operand table generated from the decompile by `re/proto/opcodes.py`]
    - Example: `float Health()` = `op1(8,0)`, `op66(4,0,4)` (copy member@0 → sp+4), `op5(0,4,4)` (copy to the return slot), `op319`.
23. **Compiler pieces exist:** `PCParser::yyparse` (yacc) and `PCScanner::GetToken` are in Core, which gives a route to compile the 6 Class objects without VMC2. `DE_CClass::SetupGlobalPCClassDefinitions` (9.6 KB) plus the factory `Init`s define the native script API.

24. **Executor model** [Core 0x7014e260 / 0x7014e580 `InternalExecute`, 0x7014eaa0]:
    - A frame is `[return][params][locals]`. A method's counters give frame size (`+0x3c`), param bytes (`+0x40`) and return bytes (`+0x44`). `IsInstant` = flag `+0x84`, `IsState` = flag `+0x90`.
    - op 1 is the prologue: it clears the return slot and locals, then suspends. Synchronous calls therefore run `ContinueExecution` twice.
    - op 305 (`exEndFrame`) suspends; op 319 returns.
    - Running off the end of the outermost frame restarts the default state, or finishes if the method is instant/has no default state. Inner frames pop to the caller; a "sync" frame returns to native code.
    - Class method index = position of the signature in the Class string pool (appended if missing).
25. **Intrinsics:** op 30 calls a native function with a pointer to its argument block (`[return][params]` for statics; `SmoothFastToStop(x)` = `1-(1-x)^2`).
    - Registration is `DE_CPCNodeClass::NewMethod(classNode, name, <Name>Caller)` plus `NewParameter` (type at `+0x3c`, 8 = float).
    - `re/native-api.tsv` lists 965 registrations (`re/proto/native_api.py`).
    - The bytecode calls 971 distinct intrinsics on 64 native classes (`re/intrinsics-used.txt`). The registrations cover 82% of call sites by name; the gaps are nested value types (`CVector`, `CText`, collision info) and `Register*State` / `Is*`.
26. **First native execution:** `engine/src/vm/executor.cpp` implements the local opcode set. Shipped `BFighterStats` bytecode (`Health`, `NormalizedHealth`, `NormalizedStamina`, `FighterLoseStamina` with branches and a parameter) runs and matches the source's math. [test `test_vm_fighter_stats`]
    - Script memory is a 32-bit arena (`vm::Memory`), because the bytecode assumes 4-byte pointers and peeks native structs at byte offsets.

27. **Images (`Image\\Standard`)** [Core 0x7018d850 `DE_CImage`, 0x70194420 `DE_CStandardImage`, 0x7018b4b0 `DE_CStandardImageCompressor::Load`, 0x7018b7a0 `SetupID`]:
    - Header: DE_CImage v5/v6 holds name, type, ResX, ResY, BOOL, float, BOOL, channel mask (v6 adds two ints).
    - Pixels are EX entries keyed by **2 raw bytes {compressor id 0, sub-image index}**, with no NUL. (The earlier key handling stripped NULs and lost index 0; fixed.)
    - Payload: `u8` version 2, a method-7 compressed block, then `[i32 channel, ResX*ResY bytes]...` terminated by −1. Channels are **planar 8-bit**: 0/1/2 colour, 3 alpha, 4 luminance.
    - Index > 0 are sub-images stored in this owner (owner/index assignment comes from the hierarchy, not yet decoded). Their size is currently inferred from the payload layout.
    - **All 2,271 images decode: 6,932 levels, 585.5 Mpx.** 38 sub-image entries on 20 images use obsolete "DZip" (method 5), which the original engine cannot read either.
    - Robot skins are 1024² with flat R/G/B regions, which look like colour-slot masks (*inference*: recoloured per pilot via `ImageFilter\\OMF2Fighter`).
28. **Meshes (`Mesh\\Standard`)** [Core 0x7012ec00, 0x7012f6b0]:
    - v6 contents hold only the counts. Geometry is in the raw EX `ObjExtCon`: chunk v1 with `u16` nverts × `f32[3]`, then `u16` nfaces × {3 × (`u16` vertex, `u16` adjacent face), `u32`}.
    - Call targets confirmed by mangled overloads (`re/scripts/CallsIn.java`). There are no UVs in the mesh; they must come from the TextureProcess/material side.
    - **All 966 meshes decode: 409,219 vertices, 692,774 triangles** (192 have no geometry). Verified visually (`re/images/JaguarTorso.png`).

29. **Hierarchy block** [Core 0x7003c110 `DE_CObject::ObjProcessHierarchy`, 0x70057a80 `ExtractChildReference`]: a v1 chunk holding a BOOL-terminated list of child references (same tag encoding, local only). This gives each file a full object tree (`deobj-dump tree`).
    - A fighter model is a `FighterModel` holding its images (RGBMain, RGBScaled, ReflectionMask, Bump), an LOD switcher, and 3 LOD meshes (`Paint1Game2500/1300/750`).
    - Each mesh holds a `TextureProcess\\MultiStage` material with `TextureManager` stages, plus 15 per-bone `DataChannel\\UnitFloats` deform weights.
30. **TextureManager** extended contents [0x70131f80] are v1: `i32` n, then n × {`u32` mesh face, 3 × (`f32` u, `f32` v)}. Each stage therefore also defines which faces it covers.
    - References [0x70131c40]: after the DE_CObject chunk, an `i32` count + image references (often external, via the GUID pool).
    - Verified visually: all 8 robots render correctly textured (`re/images/robots.png`).
31. **Pilot recolouring** [OMF 0x350350e0 `DE_COMF2FighterImageFilter::Filter`, 0x35035ff0 `DE_CColorConversion::Update`]:
    - Robot skins are R/G/B masks; out = r·C0 + g·C1 + b·C2, clamped. The `Ci` are `DE_CPilot::UserDefinedColor` slots.
    - The tables are clamp(colour·256·i, 0, 65280) >> 8. The x87 constants (256.0, 0.0, 65280.0) were read from the instruction listing (`re/scripts/ListInstructions.java`), because the decompiler hides `__ftol` operands.
    - Note: REA's own analysis truncated this function at the first `__ftol` call (the same non-returning heuristic issue as item 12), so the Ghidra project with the fix was used.
32. **Actors** [0x7008fca0 `DE_CActor::ObjProcessContents`, 0x70025f70 `DE_CCoordinateSystem`]:
    - v7 holds name, `f32` position[3], and 9 floats = an orthonormal rotation (rows), then flags. DE_CObject writes no contents chunk.
    - References [0x7008ff90]: after the DE_CObject chunk, a BOOL-terminated component list (meshes drawn by the actor).
    - Scene assembly (`engine/src/de/scene.cpp`): world = parent ∘ local, with folders as identity. Meshes = child meshes + referenced meshes, de-duplicated. Materials = MultiStage children.
    - **Arenas render natively** (`openbg-arena`): Desert, City, Iceberg, Volcano, Canyon, SpaceStation.
    - *Inference / simplification:* each material is drawn as base × 2·lightmap; the stage blend modes are not decoded yet.

33. **Skeletal animation** [Core 0x701bbf70 `DE_CAnimationSequenceGroup::ObjProcessContents`, 0x701bcd80 `DE_CStream::ProcessContents`, 0x700274c0 `DE_CQuaternion`]:
    - Bank v4: name; sequences {name; streams {`u16` channel; v1 chunk: n × {t, x, y, z}; n × {t, quat}}}; channels {name, BOOL, BOOL, `i16` parent, rest position, rest quat}.
    - Quaternions are (x, y, z, w). Time is normalised to 0..1.
    - Jaguar has 102 moves and 142 channels: the 15 bones plus cameras, grab handles, markers and an `InitialStanding*` pose.
    - The root channel `Body` carries the move's placement in the animator's scene (root motion). The viewer holds it at the origin. *Inference:* in-game it drives the fighter's movement.
34. **Skinning** [0x701201e0 `DE_CActorDeformInfo`, 0x70120d50/0x70120f50 `DE_CDeformer`, 0x701168a0 `DE_CUnitFloatsDataChannel`]:
    - DeformInfo v3 has BOOL-terminated deformers, each with a model-space bind position and rotation. The references give each deformer's bone actor.
    - Weights are mesh children `Deform<Bone>VertexData`: a UnitFloats v1 block, count 1 × element size = vertex count, one byte per vertex (0..255).
    - Linear blend skinning, `Σ w·Pose·Bind⁻¹·v` with normalised weights, reproduces coherent poses for all 8 robots (`re/images/jaguar_moves.png`, `all_kicks.png`). The blend formula is *inferred* (verified visually only).
    - Bone counts differ: Force 11, Warlord 12, Gargoyle 17 (plus a separate wing rig, `WingDeformInfo` / `WingAnimationBank`, not yet applied).
    - Playback rate per move is not decoded; the viewer uses about 1.25 s per move.

35. **Script runtime** [Core VM executor; engine `src/vm/executor.cpp`, `src/game/runtime.cpp`]:
    - Virtual calls (op 36) dispatch by vtable slot; states override methods with nested, state-scoped methods, so `ObjectSpawn` must resolve against the most-derived class (`BJaguar` → `BFighter` → `BObject`).
    - The op 1 prologue (`SetupStack`) constructs instance-typed locals (e.g. event objects) in place; leaving them null breaks `this` in later native calls.
    - Op 61 yields a per-brain "final" class instance (64 bytes here).
    - Fighter flow: `Entry` → `FighterJustCreated` (waits) → engine calls `ObjectSpawn` → `Air.FallFromSpawn` → `Air.Fall` → `Ground.Stand`. Input is switched on by a `CGenericEvent` sent to `Session` (INT1 = 1).
36. **Input sequences** [Creature 0x15006a90 `DE_CJoystickInputSequence::Verify`, 0x150020c0 `DE_CJoystickInputManager::Tick`]: the manager keeps one 8-byte record per input change {`u16` buttons, `u16` directions, `u32` age}. Tick inserts new records at age 0 and adds 1 to every older record each frame, so the counter is *frames since that input began*, not how long it was held. Verify walks requirements last-to-first against records newest-first. A record must match the buttons (`(buttons & mask) == value`) and the directions (any of the 9 bits, 0 = any). Two bounds apply: the record's own age (fields 3/5) and its *gap*, the age of the record just newer than it, i.e. how long ago it ended (fields 4/6), with 0 max = unbounded. Once a bound is met it stays met while the same requirement moves on to older matching records. One record can satisfy several consecutive requirements. A plain tap is therefore {released, gap ≤ 15} → {direction not forward, age ≤ 15} → {pressed, age ≤ 15}. Reading the counter as hold duration made old presses count as new (repeating attacks); reading it as age fixes that.
    - Buttons [OMF `Is*ButtonDownCaller`]: 0 left punch, 1 right punch, 2 left kick, 3 right kick, 4 jump, 5 duck **and** evade (one button; evading is duck + a direction).
    - `Get2DOrientation` [Creature 0x15001a20] reads the newest record's direction bits: 0 up, 1 up-right, 2 right, 3 down-right, 4 down, 5 down-left, 6 left, 7 up-left, 8 neutral. `GetWalkAction`/`GetJumpAction`/`GetPreJumpAction` [OMF 0x35027310/0x35027400/0x35027510] map those to movement actions (walk: up 1, up-left 2, up-right 3, right 4, left 5, down 8, down-left 9, down-right 10; pre-jump: forward 0x13, back 0x14, else 0x10).
37. **Fighter movement** [OMF 0x35024910 `DE_COMF2Fighter::ObjProcessContents`, 0x35027ab0 `WalkStandard`, 0x350288b0 `BeginJumpStandard`, 0x35027620 `JumpStandard`, 0x350258e0 `TurnLeft`, 0x35025af0 `Propel`, 0x35039970 `DE_CFighterPhysics::Tick`]:
    - The fighter's last contents chunk (v2) holds its movement stats as floats: turn max/step, walk speed, walk-turn max/step, back speed, back-turn max/step, air turn max/step, air forward/back push, then 6 not yet identified, then jump forward/back/up and super-jump forward/back/up. Jaguar: turn 3.5°/frame (step 0.17), walk 1.0, back −0.9, jump up 1.55, forward 0.57, back 0.4.
    - `WalkStandard` ramps the turn rate towards its max by the step, calls `TurnLeft(deg)` (positive = left), and `Propel(forward, left)` eases local momentum towards the target speed by traction × 0.1 × |target| per frame. Back speeds are scaled by 0.75.
    - `BeginJumpStandard` adds the jump's up speed to the momentum and sets the forward/back speed. In the air, gravity is 0.037 units/frame² × a multiplier that recovers towards 1 (`SetGravityRecover`). The scripts' `FPS` constant is 60.
    - Ground friction [Core 0x701dc6d0 `ApplySingleSurfaceFriction`, OMF 0x3503b1e0/0x3503a5b0]: friction = material (1) × traction; while not propelled, momentum loses 0.1 × friction of itself per frame and stops once under 0.1 × friction². No friction while propelled.
    - `Propel` cancels momentum not aligned with the wanted direction (× traction × (1 − cos)), steers up to 0.2 × traction of it onto that direction, then eases each axis. `FallStandard` is `JumpStandard`, which also adds the fighter's extra air gravity (+0x52c × 0.037) every airborne frame.
38. **Animation preludes** [Creature 0x150159c0 `DE_CCreature::AnimActivatePrelude`]: the creature captures its current pose (`AnimCaptureAsInitial`), and anim time 0..1 then blends from that pose to the new sequence at the given lead-in time. `AnimActivate` starts the sequence proper. Playing a prelude as the sequence itself made moves flicker through a whole cycle in ~10 frames.

39. **Pilots** [OMF 0x3504b450 `DE_CPilot::ObjProcessContents`, 0x3504bf50 `CalculateAIValues`, 0x3502fc70 `CVariedStat::GetValue`]:
    - Last contents chunk v4: 3 × {colour reference, f32} (90 bytes in every shipped pilot), then power/agility/endurance/focus (0-100; `Normalized*` = stat / 100). Then 31 AI stat descriptors {u8, squeezed level 0-8, variation 0-5, wave 0-3}, then 2 floats.
    - Each AI stat is a `CVariedStat`: value = base + amplitude × sin((time_ms + phase) × frequency × {0.001, 0.002, 0.004, 0.015}[wave]). Base = {0, .1, .3, .6, 1, 1.3, 1.6, 2, 3}[level]^exponent, with exponent and phase/frequency fixed per stat in `CalculateAIValues`. Amplitude = base × {0, .1, .2, .4, .7, 1}[variation]. Exceptions: attack range maps its level to {30, 50, 75, 120, 160, 220, 300, 400, 600} units; movement urgency uses {.3, .5, .75, 1, 1.3, 1.7, 2, 3.5, 6}; perception stats vary around 1 by quality.
    - AI quality degradation = clamp(1 − (difficulty + pilot offset) / 3.5, 0.01, 0.99). Difficulty is the app's setting (`DE_COMF2Manager::AIDifficulty`: 0 Rookie, 1 Amateur, 2 Veteran, 3 Champion, 4 World Champion); scripts add a per-pilot offset with `SetAIDifficultyOffset`.
    - Attack variety [`AttackAffinity` 0x3504cdd0, `TestAttackAffinity` 0x3504d0c0]: 22 usage slots, one per `aiAffinity*` bit. Affinity = mean over the flagged categories of stat / usage. Only bits 0-5, 9, 10 and 13-15 are weighed (the others only count as used). An attack fires when affinity × confidence > 1, and then each category's usage gains (quality × 0.2 + 0.7) × variation. Usage decays by 0.15 × variation whenever (time + the flagged stats' phases) / 500 ms changes, down to 1.
40. **AI commands** [Creature `DE_CCommandFactory::Init` 0x1501c6d0, `DE_CStandardHookupExecutor`]: command types navigation 2, look-at 4, observe 8, attack 0x10, acquire 0x20. The fighter registers `ExecutorAttack/MoveTo/LookAt/Aquire` through `CStandardHookupExecutor::Register*Hook(exThis(), method)`; the method value is the op 38 method handle, called back with state-scoped virtual dispatch. The hookup executor (Controllers.DEObjects) sits under the fighter's command manager, where `ResolveFighterMembers` finds it by name. The pilot (`BStandardPilot`) keeps an attack command on the best enemy. The fighter's own scripts then navigate (`NavigateForAttack`, `FindDesirableAttackPosition` 0x3502b980 → 8-direction search 0x3502b690 around the attack range) and press buttons through `CCreatureController::SetDefault*`.
41. **Script call conventions**: a native-to-script call puts the method's return slot first only if it has one; hooks like `ExecutorAttack` (no return) take their parameters from offset 0. Varparam frames (ops 312-314) stay readable after op 314 closes them; op 311 (ERROR) and op 310 (TRACE, compiled out of the shipped engine) format the most recently closed frame (types 1 string, 3 byte, 5 word, 6 int, 7 dword, 8 float, 9 bool, 10 object). Global resolvable-reference kinds are per-class container indices, not fixed types.
42. **Spawning** [Session `BStandardOMFSession`]: player spawn points are arena actors carrying `BPlayerSpawnPoint` (named PlayerSpawnPoint01...), sorted by name; slot N spawns at point N via `ObjectSpawn(position, orientation, momentum)`.

43. **Behavior interfaces** [Core 0x7013e390 `DE_CBehaviorInterface::Execute`, op 26 / 37]: an interface is (target brain, behavior). Op 26 indexes the class's *visible behaviors*: the attached behaviors in order, then, from the most-derived behavior back to the first, every behavior they reference, depth first, each once. (I recovered this by aligning op 26 operands with the source's `Interface(B...)` calls: PJaguar 9 BLaser, 13 BLauncher, 15 BFighterStats, 20 BStandardOMFSession, 35 BTargetingSight, 36/38 BCarryableLeft/Right.) A Behavior object's references are [object chunk][globals chunk][referenced behaviors chunk, BOOL-terminated][per-method entries]. `Interface(gid)` finds the brain on the object, or on one of its components (e.g. the fighter's PFighterStats brain on a child object), carrying that behavior. Op 37 calls the behavior's method number *n*, resolved virtually in the target's class: an instant method runs at once and its return value goes to the interface slot; a state method switches the target brain's state.
44. **Virtual dispatch scope**: op 36 resolves state-scoped overrides against the brain's current *state* (innermost non-instant frame), not the running instant method. Attacks override `AttackerBuildAttack` inside their state, and it is called from the instant `AttackerTryAttack`.
45. **Attacks** [OMF `DE_COMF2Manager::DE_CAttackInfo`, `DE_COMF2ManagerFactory::Init` member table]: script-visible fields at 0x44 reaction, 0x48 effect type, 0x4c attack type, 0x54 user, 0x58 block penetration, 0x5c damage, 0x60 stun, 0x64 energy, 0x68 recovery, 0x6c/0x70 diminish/retain, 0x74 momentum adjust, 0x78 guide multiplier, 0x7c connect point, 0x88/0x94 effect directions, 0xa0 transferred momentum, 0xac reel direction, 0xc0 max hits, 0xc4 flags (1 blocked, 4 super, 8 melee, 0x10 no evade, 0x20 force combo). `Prepare` gives the attack a new ID. A global hit record allows one hit per (attack ID, owner, victim). Each fighter's list of attack names (+0x490, cleared by `ResetCurrentHitList`) blocks repeats unless `m_MaxNumberOfHits` allows more. `TestForConnectedAttack` runs the collision tester and takes the first acceptable owner of a touched affector, recording the connect point.
46. **Affectors and collision** [Core `DE_CAffector`/`DE_CSphereAffector`/`DE_CConeAffector::ObjProcessContents`]: contents [actor][affector v3: u32 collision groups, BOOLs][shape]. Sphere v2 = hotspot, falloff; cone v2 = far begin, far end, near begin, near end, umbra, penumbra. Groups: 0x100000 attackable (get-hit spheres; `cgroupAttackable`), 0x1000 danger, 0x30 the body-encompassing sphere. Hit detectors hang off the skeleton's bone actors, which follow the animation pose: bone model transform = pose channel, the same transform skinning uses. Danger at a point = Σ affector strength × its CDanger level over active danger-group affectors.
47. **Fighter momentum units** [OMF `SetMomentumVectorCaller`, `SetWorldMomentumVectorCaller`, constructor +0x1dc = 16]: physics momentum is world units per frame; `SetMomentum`/`IncrementMomentum` take it as is, the `*WorldMomentum` calls take units per millisecond (×16). The floor only stops downward motion: upward momentum lifts a standing fighter off. `ForceOffFloor` clears the on-floor flag. `Dampen*Momentum(k)` scales the speed along those axes by (1 − speed × k).
48. **Blocking** [OMF `DE_CAttackInfo::TestForFighterBlock` 0x35017560, callers 0x35012d80..0x35012f10]: the attacker's script asks `AttackableIsBlocking()` (true while the defender stands, walks back, turns or holds a block/counter state) and then `TestForFighterBlock(defender, zones)`. The attack direction (owner's world Z axis; `Radiate`: owner→defender; or an explicit vector, or the transferred momentum) is taken into the defender's frame; a = −atan2(x, z); sector = trunc((a + π + π/16) / π × 8) & 15 (sector 0 = straight at its face). The attack's required-zone mask, rotated right by the sector within 16 bits, must be a subset of the defender's block mask (+0x44c, from its stats chunk after +0x528; 0 → 0xf01f, i.e. the front ±4 sectors). Non-fighters always block (`TestForBlock` sets the flag and returns TRUE). The result also sets the info's blocked flag.
49. **Op 8 global entries** [Core `DE_CClass::GlobalEntry` 0x70135fc0, `DE_CPCodeGlobal::ProcessContents/ProcessReferences`]: op 8 loads the object of global entry N, numbered consecutively over the class's behaviors in turn. Each behavior's references hold, after the object chunk, a globals chunk (v2): per entry a BOOL and, when set, the reference to its object (e.g. BFighter's DangerSphere/DangerCone templates, block-effect spawner). The contents side (v3) names each entry. Templates live outside the scene until `NewCopy` + `SetParent` puts a copy in.
50. **Momentum as scripts read it** [Core `MomentumCaller`/`MomentumLengthCaller`, `DE_CPhysics::TimeScale` +0x44]: `CActor::Momentum()` sums each physics component's momentum × its time scale; a creature sets its physics time scale to 1 / (ms per frame) = 1/16, so a fighter's `Momentum()` is units per millisecond while `SetMomentum` takes units per frame. Scripts rely on this: Katana's air Razor Spin transfers `Momentum() × 18` (≈1.1 × its frame speed), and `FighterAdjustMomentumForHit` effectively damps the victim's previous motion.
51. **Danger perception** [Creature `DE_CCreature::InternalUpdateDangerInfo` 0x150180a0, `TotalDangerLevel` 0x15018760; OMF `DE_COMFDanger::DangerLevel` 0x35066510, `IsGreatestThreatBlockable` 0x3502bb30; `DE_COMF2Fighter::FocalPoint` 0x3502ab10]: a creature samples danger once per scene time at its focal point (a fighter's `Pelvis` actor) with a zero-radius sphere test over group-0x1000 affectors: total = Σ strength × level, greatest = the largest term. An OMF danger reports 0 to its own owner actor and is blockable when its BOOL (+0x5c, default TRUE) is set. The AI (`Ground.AIUpdateAIVariables`) turns defensive when total > 0.5, the threat is blockable and its delay < 170 ms; `AIBlockGreatestThreat` then releases its buttons (standing blocks) or tries an evade or a block counter. `CUniqueActorProperty::Actor()` is the actor the property is attached to.

52. **The game session** [Session `DE_CStandardSession::Begin/Update` 0x4500e..., `DE_CStandardSessionModifier::Initialize/Begin/ObjProcessReferences` 0x45016..., `DE_CStandardSessionSlotModifier::ObjProcessReferences` 0x4501ed90; Core `DE_CSession::InitializeModifiers/BeginModifiers`]: a session is a list of modifiers, the game type's master first (Session.DEObjects `LastManStandingMaster`). Each modifier runs its own class through a ClassExecutor property (`AssureActivated`: the entry runs at once up to its first wait) and registers Begin/Update/SetAsMaster/UIManager* hooks. Modifier references: [CObject][CSessionModifier v2: two lists of description casts][CStandardSessionModifier v3: properties (executor, slot-modifier templates), then two lists of attached session modifiers]. Initialising a master copies its attached modifiers into the session (round identification, ready/fight delay, warm-up, announcer, declare-winner delay, rules display, extra time, environment picker, wait-for-fighters), which are initialised in turn. The master's Begin creates one slot per player client; each slot gets copies of the master's slot modifiers (creature and pilot picker, scoring, combo counter/display, chat relay). Slot modifier references: [CObject][v1: properties (executor), -, components attached to the creature (`FighterStats`)]. Everything else is script: generic events between session, slots and creatures, carried by `AddEventRelay` methods. A local user client is required: Last Man Standing waits while no slot is user-controlled.
53. **Descriptions** [Core `DE_CDescription::NewResource` 0x701f2170; OMF `DE_CFighterDescription::NewResource` 0x35033080]: contents hold passive references (BOOL, 0x20, file GUID, object GUID). A description's resource is its first passive reference's object; a fighter description adds its default model (`<Name>Model1` paint scheme, or the chosen one) and an extra actor (Pilots' `DefaultHUD`) under the fighter. The description's Value children go onto the resource (the "2 Wins" description's `ScoreLimit` onto `LMSScoreLimitModifier`). Pickers choose through events (`SessionSlotSelectCreature/Pilot`, Object1 = the description GUID) or UI managers (description selectors); `Construct(GUID)` instantiates a description.
54. **Loading** [BCreatureAndPilotPicker, BEnvironmentPicker scripts; the loading screen itself is engine side]: while `SessionQuerryIsLoadingRequired` (INT1 counts elements) is non-zero, every slot's `SlotQuerryUserChoicesComplete` and `SessionQuerryIsReadyToLoad` keep INT1 = 1 and the session's master has authorised loading (`SessionSlotAuthorizeForLoading`), the loading screen sends `SessionLoadElements` with INT1 = 1 once per element; the first loader waiting takes it (environment: `Construct` + `NewResource` + `OwnEnvironment`; slots: pilot and creature, `AttachComponents`, `OwnCreature`).
55. **VMC2 final instances** [Core `DE_CClass::LoadVMCode`]: after the global references: member behavior interfaces (u32 count, {u32, i32}), then the final class instances (u32 count; each a squeezed class index and the native class's own value: CColorRGBA 4 floats, CVector 3, CCoordinateSystem 9, CGUID 16 bytes, CNamedHandle a u32-length string, CText a squeezed-length string). Op 61 returns these: event handles (`g_OMFFighterDead = "FighterDead"`), colours, offsets. Generic events compare handles by name.
56. **State switches** [Core DE_CPCExecuter]: `exSleep()` (op 307) holds only the state that called it; starting a new state clears it. A state switched by a call from outside the brain (a hook, an event relay, another brain's interface call) runs its entry at once, up to its first wait: `BLastManStanding::FighterDefeated` removes the defeated fighter before its Update hook counts the living. Op 44 loads an engine class handle (the class list entry, e.g. `CEnvironmentDescription`).
57. **Passive actor references** [Core `DE_CPassiveActorReference::ObjProcessContents` 0x70092540, `ObjPrepare`, `Create` 0x700929a0]: contents v7 = passive reference, u32 +0x11c, u32 +0x120, u32 +0x124, BOOL +0x128, squeezed mode. Mode 2/3 references create their target when the scene prepares them and give it their name, transform and children (arena spawn points, pickup spawners, light placements are such references to SharedObjects actors).
58. **Pilots** [OMF `DE_CPilot::AttackBestEnemy` 0x3504d370, `Attack`, `ControlledCreatureCaller`; Creature `DE_CCreature::IsAlive`]: a pilot (Pilots.DEObjects `Actor\Creature\Pilot`, under its fighter) runs `BStandardPilot`, which picks a target each frame with `AttackBestEnemy`: the nearest creature with global interest > 0 (the current target's distance weighed by how long it has been the target), then `CommandAttack(target, aggression)`. `IsAlive` = global interest != 0 (fighters set it with `SetGlobalInterest`).

59. **Engine-built objects files** [Core.ModuleDLL resources, type "DEOBJECTS"]: Core carries two whole objects files, `COREMeshes` (id 104: fonts, primitive meshes) and `COREImages` (id 106: UI and joystick images), which `ConstructCoreMesh` and the transfer agent load by name ("Missing Default Font [Font_Ariel] from COREMeshes TransferAgent").
60. **Flat-text fonts** [Core `DE_CFlatText3Factory::CFontInfo::CreateFont` 0x701c9...]: a font is a COREMeshes mesh (`Font_Ariel`, `Font_Metalic`) of 512 triangles; faces pair with the next face sharing a vertex into 256 letter quads, sorted by their left edge, so quad n draws character n (plain ASCII order). Each letter keeps its mesh extent and texture box; heights are relative to the lowest letter (`CLetter::FixUp`); the font height is the tallest extent.
61. **Screen-space primitives** [Core `DE_CPrimitiveGeneratorMesh`, `DE_CFlatText3::CalcScreenCoordinates`, `CStatusBarPrimitive::UpdateBar` 0x7012d210]: in a screen-space mesh, positions are fractions of the screen with **y up** (top alignment puts text at y = 1 − height); text y is its baseline. Quads map (x0, y0)-(x1, y1) to UV (u0, v0)-(u1, v1) with image rows top to bottom. A status bar fills from its begin point to begin + (end − begin) × value with the texture's lower half and draws a cap one bar-thickness either side of that point with the upper half. New `CColorRGBA` instances are opaque white. The HUD (`BDefaultHUD`, under each fighter via its description's extra actor) draws the score panel, pilot photos (`CPilot::CurrentPhoto` = the pilot's first image), names, stats, health/stun/energy bars and the current target.
62. **Fight camera** [Fighter.DEObjects `FighterCamera` + `PFighterCameraMover`; Core transform callers]: each fighter's `FighterViewRotatingCamera` passive reference becomes a `FighterCamera` actor whose script places it behind and above the fighter (55 back, 38 up, pitched 12 degrees down) and, with a target in front, raises it, moves it in and tilts it towards the target (`SlideTo` easing), plus rumble from forces. Its `CameraActor` child is the view; `New Camera` holds the field of view (70.5), near (10) and far (100000). `LocalOrientation()` returns the actor's own orientation; `CCoordinateSystem::RotateX/Y/Z` rotate every axis about the parent axis, `RotateLocal*` about the actor's own axis, `MoveLocal*` along the actor's axes, `SlideTo(a, b, t)` interpolates clamped.

63. **Small fixes from the placeholder list** [Core `DE_CScene::CIncidentRecorder` 0x70072cf0..0x70072f10, `NewIncidentRecorder` 0x70075840; `CFontInfo::CFontInfo` 0x701c9110, `CLetter::FixUp`, `CFontInfo::Update`, `TextSetShadowOnCaller` 0x7012ba80; OMF `DE_COMF2Manager::Activate`, `Set{Movement,Turn}SpeedMultiplierCaller`, `DE_COMF2Fighter::GlobalInterest` 0x3502ae40, `PowerupLikelihood` 0x3502c030]:
    - An incident recorder keeps (time, type) incidents; `AddIncidentCurrentTimeIfAbsent` adds one only when none of that type lies within the threshold (default 100 ms; incidents older than 300 s are dropped). The fighter throttles hit sounds, throw damage, projectiles and effects with them; pickup spawners their spawns.
    - Combos end when the victim's `FighterRecoverFromBeingHit` sends `FighterRecover` (on returning to a stand state), not on a timer.
    - `FONT_DEFAULT` is "Font_Ariel", `FONT_IN_GAME_DEFAULT` "Font_Metalic". A letter advances by its width (`FixUp` stores x1 − x0) plus the letter spacing (+0x9c, default 0). A text primitive's shadow is offset 0.06 font units right and down, black at 0.5 alpha.
    - The AI difficulty is the registry value `AIDifficulty` (created with 0, Rookie).
    - +0x4bc multiplies the walk/run propel speed and +0x4c0 the turn angle (both 1 by default).
    - A fighter's global interest is its own (+500), x (900 − d + 1) for each enemy within 900 units, refreshed every 300 ms.
    - `PowerupLikelihood` is the likelihood `FindPowerupToAquire` reports through its float reference (it walks the creature's needs).
    - Render-tick hooks take the milliseconds since the last drawn frame.

64. **Sounds** [Core `DE_CStreamNoise`, `DE_CScene::PlayNoiseSphere*` / `PlayNoiseAmbient`]: `Noise2\Stream` contents v3 = [name][volume][frequency][frequency variance][alternate chance %]; the second reference list holds alternates (one is picked with the alternate chance); the samples are a RIFF PCM WAVE in the `DeNoiseSampleExDataId` EX entry. A sphere noise is at full volume within its hotspot and falls linearly to silence at the falloff; the played rate is frequency × noise frequency × (1 + CenterRand(variance)).
65. **Particles** [Core `DE_CParticleGenerator` 0x701d5540/0x701d5b00, `DE_CParticleGeneratorInstance::SetTime` 0x701d5e70, `DE_CParticleModifier` 0x701d7970 / `CommonSpawnerManageParticles` 0x701d7d40, `DE_CManualParticleSpawner` 0x701d3cb0..0x701d42c0, `DE_CParticleRenderer::Process` 0x701c30a0; Particles module spawners/modifiers and `DE_CBillboardedQuadParticleRenderer` 0x40010160 / 0x40010620 / 0x400119b0, `DE_CRingParticleRenderer` 0x400158d0; Core `DE_CValueInterFLOAT` 0x7010d1a0, interpolation types 0x7010a270..0x7010c8d0, `DE_CValueModulatedRGBA` 0x701117d0]:
    - An effect is an actor whose mesh is a particle renderer; the renderer references its images and a `ParticleGenerator`, which lists its modifiers. The renderer makes one generator instance per actor that draws it and steps it to the scene time when drawn. Each step: the instance takes the actor's world placement (world-space generators, `+0x4c` = 0); every modifier manages particles (spawners create them); if any were created every modifier initialises the new ones; ages advance by dt / life span (expired ones die unless looping) and momentum (units per ms) moves them; then every modifier modifies the live ones. The step is capped.
    - A particle is a 0x34-byte header (position, spawn position, age 0..1, age step, spawn fraction, size, serial, flags 2 relative / 4 alive / 8 new / 0x10 looping, 1 / life span) plus optional channels: momentum (12), orientation (36), colour (16), channel 3, channel 4 "intensity" (4), rotation in turns (4).
    - Modifier base v4: [name][spawn time ± variation][life span ± variation][spawn size ± variation][BOOL spawning][BOOL looping][initial count][interpolate actor motion][extra per spawn]. Spawning counts down by dt and emits until the countdown is positive again, each particle's life 1 / (life + Rand(variation) + 1); the initial count and `Spawn(n)` bursts come out at once. `SpawningOn/Off` flip the modifier's flag; `CActor::Enable/DisableParticleSpawning` the flag of the instances that actor draws. `MATH::Rand(x)` = 0..x, `MATH::CenterRand(x)` = −x/2..x/2 (also the script `CenterRandom`).
    - Spawner placement: ellipsoid = random direction × Rand(half extents) + centre; box = uniform (v3 can wrap particles that leave it, per axis); mesh = a vertex (random, cycling, backwards, or once through; its own burst puts one particle on every vertex). World-space positions are rotated into the actor's frame and moved to its position (interpolated by the spawn fraction when asked). The manual spawner takes queued requests: `Spawn(size, life)` at the actor, `Spawn(vector, …)`, `Spawn(vector, orientation, …)`, `Spawn(actor, …)` at that actor's world position; `SetLastIntensity` / `SetLastRotation` set the last request's channel values. A fighter's damage flash is `Spawn(connect point, 1, 290)` on `StandardDamageEffect`, the block flash `Spawn(connect point, 1.4, 350)`.
    - New-particle modifier v1: rotation (Rand(1)), orientation (from/towards the actor, random, actor momentum, actor axes), momentum (scene gravity (0, −0.0098, 0) × k, along the orientation's Z, CenterRand(k), (pos − actor) × k, radiate normalize(pos − actor) × k), optionally plus the actor's momentum capped and scaled, set or added. Momentum-and-acceleration v4 initialises momentum from the actor's momentum per local axis + CenterRand + fixed, and accelerates by mode (back to the spawn point, gravity 9.6e-6 × amount, swirl, attraction...). Gravity: momentum.y −= dt × strength × 9.6e-6. Wind: momentum eases to the scene wind (zero) over `strength` seconds. Miscellaneous functions: a FLOAT curve over age drives the size (0) or rotation (1).
    - Billboard renderer v7: [colour value][streak length +0x6c][mode +0x88][UV mode +0x8c][colour mode +0x90][width, height][normalise][offset x, y][cone mode][glow value][z offset][+0x84]. Modes: 0 screen-aligned square of half-size size × width/height; 13 the same turned by the rotation channel; 6/16/17 a camera-facing quad along the orientation's Z/X/Y axis, 7/9/11 along the actor's Z/X/Y (rain streaks); 1–5 streaks along a direction × the streak length. UV mode 1 is a 4×4 flipbook by age, 2 a 4×4 frame by serial. Colour modes: 1/2 × age / 1 − age, 3 × intensity, 4/5 alpha × age / 1 − age, 7 the colour value at the age, 8 × the colour channel. Blend 0/1 premultiply by alpha (`DE_CRGBA::NormalizeAlpha`) and add; 2 alpha-blends.
    - Ring renderer v1: inner/outer colour values, plane mode, detail and FLOAT curves over age (× size) for the inner and outer edge heights and radii.
    - Values: `Value\FLOAT` [name][float]; `Value\FLOAT\Inter` v4 adds min, max, the interpolation type's name ("Lerp Type", "SmoothStep Type", "Sin Type", "Quarter Circle Type", "Gamma Type", "Bias Type", "Gain Type", "Spline Type", "f(x) = coth(x)sin(x)/sec(x) Type"), its contents ([v3 reverse, mirror, peak][type parameter or spline points]) and a frequency (t = frac(t × f)). `ModulatedRGBA` = a colour value with alpha × a FLOAT value. An "advanced reference" embeds a default value in the contents and may point at a Value object in the references.
66. **Attack trailers** [OMF `DE_CAttackTrailer` 0x3503fa90 `GeneratePoint`, 0x35040250 `Process`, `RampToward` 0x3503ff00, `DE_COMF2Fighter::ObjPrepare` (Setup calls), `Tick` 0x350253a0]: a fighter owns four trailers (left/right punch, left/right kick) whose emitters are its `<Left|Right><Punch|Kick>TrailerEmitter` object references, with offset and half-width vectors from its contents (+0x540..+0x59c, after the extra air gravity) and its `AttackTrailer` image. Each fighter tick a trailer adds a point (emitter position + offset, rotated half-width, current colour) while the script called `Continue()` that frame; otherwise it fades its colour to black over 8 points and keeps adding while the colour is above 0.01. `SetupLight/Medium/Hard(Heavy)Attack` ramp to (0.1, 0.1, 0.3) over 8 points and give points 20/30/50 ticks of life; `SetColor` / `SetDestColor` ramp to a colour. Consecutive points born within 2 ticks are joined by an additive quad; dead points leave from the front.
67. **Smaller natives** [Core `ConvertFromWorldToScreenCaller` 0x701833b0, `RandomINTCaller`, `CenterRandomCaller`]: `ConvertFromWorldToScreen` gives x, y as screen fractions (camera-space x / z / (2 tan(fov/2)) + 0.5, y scaled by the aspect) and z the view depth; the HUD hides the target's floating bars when z < 10. `Random(int)` is 0..n−1. `RegisterDeltaTickHook` hooks run every scene tick with its length in ms. `CValueFLOAT::GetValue(t)` evaluates the value's curve.

68. **Forces and materials** [Core `DE_CAffector::ApplyForce` 0x7007b2f0, `DE_CCollisionResults::ApplyForce` 0x7007bde0, `DE_CMaterial::ProcessEvent`/`ProcessForce` 0x7020bb10..0x7020bd40, `IsValidSurface`, `DE_CCommonForce::Strength` 0x7020d010 (disassembled), `StrengthAtPosition`, `Tick` 0x7020d2a0, `ObjPrepare`, `DE_CCommonForceParticleSpawningMaterial::ProcessForce`, `DE_CParticleSpawningMaterial::SpawnSingleParticle`, `DE_CSingleCollisionInfo::Material` 0x7007c2f0, `DE_CMesh::GetElementInfo` 0x70127bd0]:
    - `ApplyForce(force, groups)` collision-tests the affector against active affectors in `groups` and sends each touched one a collision event carrying the force (+0x44 results, +0x48 force, +0x4c index); returns whether anything was touched. The touched affector's materials (its +0x20 properties) process it.
    - Collision info (stride 0x3c): +0 touched affector, +8 push-back normal, +0x14 depth, +0x18 the surface's own normal, +0x24 touch point, +0x30 mesh face. A mesh affector reports one info per face the sphere reaches.
    - `DE_CMaterial` contents v3: element name (data block), incident threshold (ms, a scene incident recorder keyed by the touched affector's owner), value +0x6c (traction: HighTraction 3, LowTraction 1, Glass 0.4, Slippery 0.005, NoTractionHazard −1). With an element name it covers only mesh faces whose byte data channel of that element is set. Channel (`DataChannel\Generic`) contents: [name, type "Byte", element name, u32][u32 1, u32 count, one byte per face]. `CSingleCollisionInfo::Material` = the first of the affector's materials that covers the face. Hooks (`RegisterProcessForceHook`) run with (force, touch point, push-back normal).
    - `ParticleSpawningMaterial` v3: spawn mode +0x88 (0 at the point, 1 oriented by the normal and pushed out by size × offset +0x8c); references: renderer (+0x74, added to the actor's properties at prepare) and manual spawner (+0x80).
    - `CommonForceParticleSpawningMaterial` v3: size range +0xa0/+0xa4, strength range +0xa8/+0xac, particle size +0xb0/+0xb4 and life +0xb8/+0xbc interpolated over them, intensity at the lowest strength +0xc0, type mask +0x9c (force +0x54 must meet it), BOOL +0xc4, 4 floats; references: actor to create (+0x94, copied to the touch point). Forces outside either range do nothing (hooks included).
    - `CommonForce` v2: type +0x54, strength (advanced reference), size +0x6c, mode +0x50, rate +0x70. Strength: mode 0 the value, 1 value × |actor momentum|, 2 value × (1 + |momentum|) (momentum per ms). `StrengthAtPosition` = the actor affector's intensity there (falloff profile × its "Intensity" value) × strength. With a rate the force ticks itself every `rate` ms, testing its affector against group 0x200.
    - Types in the data: 0x10 footfalls and bullets, 0x20 a fighter's body (PlayerForce), 0x80 rumble, 3 hits. Groups: 0x200000 fighting surfaces (`cgroupFightingSurface`; the arena surface, ramps and the mine field), 0x200 force receivers (jump pads, death volume, the cameras' rumble spheres).
    - `UniqueActorProperty` references to another file (Forces.DEObjects' FootfallsForce) are per actor: each actor's own copy, its `Actor()` that actor.
69. **Fighter surface physics** [OMF `DE_CFighterPhysics::Tick` 0x35039970, `UpdateCollisionData` 0x3503ad60, Core `DE_CSimplePhysics::UpdateForFriction` 0x701dc280, `PerformCollisionTests`, `DE_COMF2Fighter::IsNavigable` 0x3502b8f0, `IsStandingState` 0x35029ad0]:
    - The fighter's `Physics\Simple\Fighter` property tests its first test affector (references chunk 2: FloorCollisionAffector, an 8-unit sphere 8 units up) against groups +0x94 (0x200000).
    - Tick: momentum capped at 10/frame; position += momentum; momentum += acceleration (gravity × 0.037 down while airborne, else 0); collision; momentum loses 0.1 × (direction · normal) of its normal part per contact facing it; push against the other fighter; a fighter that just walked off the floor in a `Ground()` state probes 2 units down; floor change fires LeaveFloor (event type 4) / TouchFloor (5) recipients; on the floor surface friction (material value × traction) else gravity; then every touched surface (once per owner) takes the fighter's +0x5a0 force (PlayerForce) with strength 10 × the speed into it.
    - Collision data: own affectors dropped; the floor contact's material value scales the wall test: value × normal.y ≤ 0.75 is a wall (summed normals; moving into them faster than 0.1 calls the hit-wall hooks with that speed), else floor when the push-back and surface normals agree (> 0.9999) and the fighter isn't moving away. Push-out along the floor's height only, along the push-back normal otherwise, to the deepest contact's depth.
    - `IsNavigable(a, b)`: the segment's first surface (groups +0x5dc = 0x200000) must face up more than 0.75 and have no material of value ≤ 0.
    - A `Physics\Simple` with a force (FloorCollisionAffector's MaterialForceApplicator) tests its actor every tick against its groups (+0x94, 0x200) and applies the force to every contact: PlayerForce reaches jump pads (BMaterialBasedLauncher), mine fields (BMines) and the death volume (BDeathByMaterial) this way.
70. **Passive reference flags and killing effects** [Core `DE_CPassiveActorReference::Create` 0x700929a0, Particles `DE_CKillWithParticles::Modify` 0x40003da0]: contents v7 after the GUIDs: u32 +0x11c (placement: 0 = the target's own transform carried by the reference's), u32 +0x120 (add the created actor to the scene when it has no parent: see 96), u32 +0x124 (deactivate it: actor vtable +0x128 is `Deactivate`), BOOL +0x128 (give it the reference's name; else it keeps the target's), squeezed mode. Fighters' destruction explosion and spark references are deactivated templates; the arena's spawner references keep their target names (`LaunchableAttachedActorSpawner`). The created actor also takes the reference's properties. `KillWithParticles`: a countdown from 5000 ms (+0x74); once it has run out and the instance has no particles, every object in its references list is deleted (the effect's own actor).

71. **VM: interface owners and state virtual tables** [Core `DE_CPCExecuter::ContinueExecutionInternal` 0x7014eaa0]: op 24 is `Owner(interface)`, the object owning the brain behind the interface (not the behavior's global id). A state's compiled method carries its own per-slot table (`base_methods`, `inherit` included), so a virtual call made while a state (or a state enclosing the running method) is active resolves through that table first; `v78` is the `DoesInherit` target. Case 0x22 calling a state from an instant method clears the instance's main executer, `SetState`s it and ends the calling (per-call) executer: an interface call whose callee switches state returns without running the rest of the method.
72. **Creatures and simple physics** [Creature `DE_CCreature` contents, `SceneTicksPerUpdate`, `SetCreationTime`; Core `DE_CSimplePhysics` 0x701dc...]: creature contents chunk 1 (v2–4) begins with u32 `SceneTicksPerUpdate` (fighters 16, spawners 100, the jet 33): the brain pulses once per that many scene ms; `SetCreationTime` aligns down to it. Simple physics base chunk v2: name, BOOL enabled, int rate. Rate 0 runs `DeltaTick` every scene update (momentum in units/ms, gravity 0.037/256 per ms²); rate > 0 runs `Tick` every `rate` ms.
73. **Affector owners** [Core `DE_CAffector` +0x118]: references chunk 2, list 0 = [owner, encompassing]; `OwnerActor()` is that owner, else the affector itself. Copies of one template share the reference, so the owner is the nearest copy (the affector's own branch, then each ancestor's): two spawners each holding a jet pickup must each see their own jet.
74. **Navigation links** [Creature `DE_CNavigationLink::CPath` `CalculatePath` 0x1500ef90, `GetMoveToPosition` 0x1500f8b0, `StepTo` 0x1500f5a0, `PreStepCalculate` 0x1500f790; `DE_CMoveToCommand::Update` 0x15021640]: areas are sphere affectors under `NavParent` with `NavigationLink` properties (references chunk 2: the destination area). Wavefront over open links: cost = walked + the destination area's distance to the goal; each pass expands links within 50 of the cheapest; the path is the chain back to the start. `GetMoveToPosition` drops areas as the creature enters them; paths are recalculated at most every 500 ms (only a found path resets the timer). `GetPathAffector` grows a sphere 1..500 until it meets an area.
75. **Local rotations** [Core `DE_CActor::RotateLocalX/Y/ZDeg`]: R(−a) in our parent-axis convention, so positive local X pitches the nose up (the fighter jets dive with negative angles).
76. **Grapples** [OMF `AnimHeadFollow`, `AnimCenterOfGravityFollow`, `AnimPrepareHeadAnchorForPreludeToFollow`, `CActor::RotateToFace`]: the victim's pelvis is placed so its `HeadAnchor` meets the thrower's follow target (`rel = pelvis⁻¹·HeadAnchor`, pelvis world = target·rel⁻¹), or at the target for centre-of-gravity follows; `PrepareHeadAnchor…` moves the anchor onto the target for this frame. `RotateToFace(actor, f)` yaws by atan2(dx, dz), 1/f of the way. Fighter-vs-fighter push uses each fighter's `OtherFighterPushSphere` (5.5): pushed back by no more than the distance moved this tick + 0.1, 5% of the momentum into the other removed; `SkipFighterCollision` disables it for a tick.
77. **Passive reference properties** [Core `DE_CPassiveActorReference::Create` 0x700929a0]: the created actor's properties are the target's plus the reference's own (references chunk 1 list 0). Jaguar's `ConcussionBoltRef` gives its `ProjectileActor` the `EConcussionBolt` executor; SharedObjects' test lights get `RenderActorRotatorExecutor`; Pickups' `SmallEnergyBoost` an `EnergyValue`.
78. **Creature owner** [OMF `SetOwnerCaller` 0x35012570, `OwnerCaller` 0x35012590, `DE_COMF2Fighter::SetAsNewProjectileOwner` 0x350213b0]: `CCreature::SetOwner`/`Owner()` keep the creature that made this one (a projectile's fighter: `BStandardProjectile` skips victims whose owner is its own). `SetAsNewProjectileOwner(p)` makes the fighter the owner of an `OMF2Projectile`.
79. **Needs** [Creature `DE_CCreature::InternalUpdateNeedInfo` 0x15018400, `AssureNeedInfoUpdated` 0x150186e0, `GreatestNeed` 0x15008460, `CommandAquire` 0x15017e80; OMF `DE_COMFNeed::DesireLevel` 0x350679a0, `ObjProcessContents` 0x35067790, `ObjPrepare` 0x35067930, `DE_COMF2Fighter::FindPowerupToAquire` 0x3502bb70, `PowerupLikelihood` 0x3502c030; COMF2Fighter vtable 0x350795f0, slots +0x1c4 GreatestNeed, +0x1cc FindPowerupToAquire, +0x1d0 PowerupLikelihood]:
    - `UniqueActorProperty\Need\OMF` contents: [UniqueActorProperty][DE_CNeed v1, empty][v1, 60 bytes: five floats read into +0x50, +0x58, +0x5c, +0x60, +0x54, then padding]. Values: `TeamIndex` (+100, the need's or its holder's, else −1), `Exclusive` object reference (+0x68), BOOL `Area` (+0x6c).
    - Data: jet, nuke and lightning needs +0x50 = 1; the energy boost +0x58 = 1; the football +0x5c = 2, +0x60 = 1; its destination +0x50 = +0x5c = +0x60 = 1; each fighter's own `NeedAffector` need +0x60 = 1.
    - `DesireLevel(flags, creature)`: 0 for a creature other than the Exclusive one, for whoever already carries it (unless Exclusive), and for the holder itself. With Area the holder affector scales by the creature's position and a TeamIndex other than the creature's zeroes it. For a fighter with a pilot: flags & 8 → AffinityDefendObjective (+0x688) × +0x54; flags & 2 → max(AffinityCarryable (+0x5a8) × +0x5c, AffinityPowerup (+0x5c8) × +0x58, AffinityPowerup × +0x50); flags & 4 → + 1.5 × +0x60 when the holder is a fighter and an enemy.
    - Need info (once per scene time): a 100000-unit sphere test in group 0x800; each touched actor's Need property with desire (all flags) > 0 is known (+0x168); the greatest (the previous greatest × 1.3) is `GreatestNeed` (+0x158), its desire `GreatestNeedDesire` (+0x2e4). A fighter's greatest need is usually the enemy's `NeedAffector` (1.5), which raises its move urgency (it runs).
    - `FindPowerupToAquire(threshold&)`: each known need's desire with flags 2, for the actor holding it (an affector's owner); 0 if the fighter already holds it; a `BCarryableRight`/`Left` with that hand (+0x324 / +0x318) free × AffinityCarryable, else 0; then × 1/((d + 6.5)/50)² when that is above 1. The best wins if it beats 2 × threshold (threshold set to it), else null and threshold 0. `PowerupLikelihood` = the best with threshold 0.
    - `CommandAquire(actor, urgency)`: a `DE_CAquireCommand` (urgency +0x78) that calls the creature's acquire hook (`ExecutorAquire`): the fighter walks up and ducks within 20 to pick it up.
80. **Running** [OMF `DE_COMF2Fighter::RunStandard` 0x35028330; contents +0x4c4..+0x4d0]: run speed +0x4c4 (default 2.6), running-turn speed +0x4c8 (0.6 × run after loading), running turn max +0x4cc (1) and step +0x4d0 (0.16). Action 11 runs straight (speed = factor × run, turn 0); 12/13 run turning left/right (speed = factor × turning speed, turn eased towards ± factor × max by the step); then `TurnLeft(turn)` and `Propel(speed × movement multiplier)`.
81. **Reference members** [Core `DE_CSingleReference::RefNotifyDestroyingTarget`]: script object members assigned through ops 62/64 are engine references: destroying the target nulls them (a fighter's `m_LeftHandCarryActor` once its jet pickup deletes itself).

82. **Danger maps** [Creature `DE_CDanger::ObjProcessContents` 0x1502d930 / `ObjInitContents`; OMF `DE_COMFDanger::DangerLevel` 0x35066510; Core `DE_CBoxAffector::IsTouching(point, &intensity)` 0x7007e520, `DE_CValueFLOATImageMap::GetValue` 0x7010fe40..0x7010fe70, `ObjProcessContents` 0x7010fbc0, `DE_CImage::GetNormalizedPixel` 0x7018e4b0]: a danger property's v1 chunk holds its level (+0x50, default 1) and delay (+0x54); `DE_COMFDanger::DangerLevel` is 0 for the affector's own owner. A box affector's intensity at a point inside it is its intensity value (+0x180, references chunk 3 list 0) at the box-normalised position, else 1 (0 outside). `Value\FLOAT\ImageMap`: [CValueFLOAT v1: scale +0x2c][v1: plane mode +0x3c: 0 = (x, y), 1 = (x, z), 2 = (y, z)], references chunk 3: the image; the value is the image's luminance (channel 4) at (u × width, v × height), clamped, / 255, × the scale. Desert's `FightingSurfaceDanger` (a 1500 × 2000 × 2500 box, group 0x1000, level 1) maps danger 1 over the minefields and slopes off the cross-shaped plateau, which is what turns the AI's `ExecutorMoveTo` (danger sampled 80 ahead and ±45°) away from the edges.
83. **Running for the player** [OMF `DE_COMF2Fighter::GetRunAction` 0x35027a10]: controller orientation up → 11 (run straight), up-left or left → 12, up-right or right → 13, otherwise 0 (stop).

84. **Material stages** [Core `DE_CMultiStageTextureProcess::ObjProcessReferences` 0x701190d0, `Process` 0x7011a5f0, `CStage::Process` 0x7011a8a0; Direct3D `DE_CShader::CStage::Setup` 0x20031760, `SetStates` 0x20031b60, `DE_CGenericShader::AddStage` 0x20033500, `ActivateAndDrawPatches` 0x20032ed0]:
    - References: [DE_CObject][DE_CTextureProcess v1][MultiStage, v8 in every shipped file: i32 count, per stage {texture manager, BOOL colour-is-reference (else RGBA floats), i32 mode, BOOL stacked +0x2c, i32 process +0x4c, BOOL +0x30, BOOL disabled +0x34}]. v1: {manager, mode name, RGBA}. Mode names: Replace/Opaque 0, Add 1, Modulate 2, AddToLightMap 3 (reported as unsupported, drawn as Add), SemiTranslucent 4.
    - Each stage becomes a render material element (+0x30): 0 → 1 opaque, 1/3 → 3 add, 2 → 2 modulate, 4 → 4 translucent; non-first elements draw after the base (placement 6). A stacked stage shares the previous stage's pass; when it cannot be drawn (a reflection the mesh does not take) the pair is skipped.
    - Frame blend of a pass (SRCBLEND +0x30, DESTBLEND +0x2c): opaque (ONE, ZERO), modulate (DESTCOLOR, INVSRCALPHA), add (SRCALPHA, ONE), translucent (SRCALPHA, INVSRCALPHA).
    - Texture stages: the first is the texture (× the flat colour through TFACTOR when not white, × the vertex colour when lit); stacked stages combine with the current colour: replace SELECTARG1, modulate MODULATE, add ADD, translucent MODULATE. A modulating stage whose image has alpha (image flag 8) uses TEXTURE|ALPHAREPLICATE, so masks blend as lerp(dst, dst × tex, alpha). An opaque first stage with alpha is alpha-tested (ALPHAREF 8, GREATEREQUAL). Texture coordinate generation 3 (camera-space reflection vector through the renderer's view transform) serves `TextureManager\Reflection`.
    - Common stage layouts: `T0` (413 `T0 T1 R2*`: base, then an added mask × the environment), `T0 T2` (base × lightmap pass), lightmap base + AddToLightMap, then multiplied colour/detail passes.
85. **Environment cube map** [Creature `DE_CEnvironment::ObjProcessReferences` 0x1500b7f0, Direct3D `DE_CStandardRenderer::CubeEnvironmentImage`]: a `TextureManager\Reflection` without its own image samples the environment's cube image (+0x39c), built from the environment's v5 references: [RGBA advanced reference (a v1 chunk: BOOL, reference)][seven BOOL + image slots +0x320, +0x338, +0x32c, +0x308, +0x314, +0x344, +0x350][RGBA][FLOAT]. Desert's six are EnvironmentImageCube Top, Front, Bottom, Left, Right, Back in slot order.
86. **The arena is not a run-time copy to draw twice**: the arena description's `NewResource` copies the whole arena tree into the scene; those copies are the static scene, not run-time actors (drawing both doubled every multiplying pass).

87. **Fighter paint** [OMF `DE_COMF2FighterImageFilter::Filter` 0x350350e0, `DE_CColorConversion::Update` 0x35035ff0, `UpdateStaticColorRemapperTable` 0x35036150, `ObjInitContents` 0x35034da0; `DE_CPilot::UserDefinedColor` 0x3504c8e0, `ObjProcessContents` 0x3504b450]: the filter finds its fighter (the first `OMF2Fighter` ancestor) and takes the pilot's three user-defined colours (+0x368 + k × 0x18: CValueRGBA advanced references, each followed by an f32, at the start of the pilot's v4 chunk). Per colour a 256-entry table of i × c × 256 clamped to 0..65280; a pixel's R, G, B index colours 0, 1, 2 and the three entries are summed and shifted by 8, clamped to 255 (24- and 32-bit images; alpha untouched). An image named `ReflectionMask` takes the colours lightened, normalize(c × 0.5 + 0.5). Defaults before a pilot: (1, 0.88, 0.8), (0.36, 0.56, 0.48), (0.48, 0.96, 1.28). Static images (+0x48) are added through a remap table of i × clamp(0.7 × Σ colour·weights + 0.3), scaled ones (+0x34) added directly; both lists are empty in the data.
88. **Mesh lighting flags** [Core `DE_CMesh::ObjProcessContents` 0x701275e0; Direct3D `DE_CRendererDirect3D::CreateNewMesh` 0x20025610, `DE_CD3DRenderMeshElement::InitialSetup` 0x2002e550]: DE_CMesh +0x40 (base chunk v3+, bit 0x1000 cleared): 0x08 lit, with 0x10 D3D vertex lighting (element flag 1: shader flag 8, `LIGHTING`/`COLORVERTEX`, ambient material from the vertex colour), without it lit by light projectors (element flags 6: the touched-polygon list); 0x20 projected-texture target. Arena geometry is mostly unlit or projector-lit; fighters, vehicles and pickups are vertex-lit.
89. **Lights** [Core `DE_CLight::ObjProcessContents` 0x701baf70, `GlobalLightMap` 0x701bae30; Direct3D `DE_CStandardRenderLight::SetLight` 0x2000f510, `UpdateLitActors` 0x2000f720, `DE_CD3DLight::SetupComplete`/`Activate`/`Update`, `DE_CStandardRenderAffector::Update` 0x20006570 / `Intensity` 0x20006fa0, `DE_CStandardProjectorShader::SetupSphereProjector` 0x20034350 / `DrawPatches` 0x20033b20 / `SetStates` 0x20033830]: Light v5 = [name][RGBA advanced reference][f32 intensity][i32 mode][i32 dynamic]; mode → actor mask 0:6, 1:4, 2:0x10, 3:8. A sphere-affector light (type 7) and a cone one (type 1) are D3D point lights (range 100000, attenuation 1, 0, 0) whose diffuse is colour × intensity × the affector's intensity at the lit mesh's actor: sphere 1 inside the inner radius (+0x184), linear to 0 at the outer (+0x17c); cone linear from far-begin to far-end along local +Z and between the cosines of umbra and penumbra (degrees). Projector passes draw the touched polygons again, additive (ONE, ONE), TFACTOR = clamp(colour × intensity), textured by the core image `LightMap` (a 32 × 32 radial fade): a sphere samples it twice through texture matrices over its XZ and XY planes scaled by 1 / (2 × outer); a cone projects it through the cone.
90. **Ambient** [Creature `DE_CEnvironment::ObjProcessContents` 0x1500b550 / `ObjPrepare` 0x1500bb10; Direct3D `DE_CStandardRenderer::UpdateScene`, `DE_CRendererDirect3D::Render`]: the environment's v2/v3 chunk holds an RGBA advanced reference (ambient, 0.3 grey by default) and an f32 scale; ObjPrepare copies them to the scene (+300..+0x138), and the D3D ambient render state is colour × scale (white when the renderer is full-bright).
91. **Stage colour channels** [Core `DE_CTextureManager::Process` 0x701327c0]: a texture manager's `DataChannel\UnitFloats` children fill its colour element (one RGBA per face corner, white by default); a "ColorRGB" channel stores [i32 components = 3][i32 entries = faces × 3] then bytes. With D3D lighting the vertex colour is the ambient and diffuse material colour.
92. **Glow layer** [Direct3D `DE_CShader::GetShader` 0x20032170, `DE_CGenericShader::ActivateAndDrawPatches` 0x20033286.., `DE_CRendererDirect3D::SetupGlowRenderTarget` 0x20028e50, `UpdateGlowMaps` 0x20026ce0, `SuperSampleForGlow` 0x200283b0, `Downsample4x4` 0x200280c0, `DrawGlow` 0x20028870; HLSL in the Direct3D module's strings]: a mesh with a `GlowFactor` child value (devices with the glow option) gets a second shader in layer 9 (flag 0x400): BLENDFACTOR = grey(min(0.25 × factor, 1)), black D3DFOG_EXP fog of density 0.0007 (0.7 for AddToLightMap passes). A pass with a reflection stage (element property 0x100 from `DE_CReflectionTextureManager::Process`) gets one with flags 0xc00 when the environment has a `HighRangeSpecular` cube child: factor = the last stage's `SpecularFactor` (1 without one), no fog, the specular cube. `UpdateGlowMaps` draws layer 9 into a 256 × 256 target with its own depth, `SuperSampleForGlow` adds three passes of an 8-tap average (`Color*=(1.0/8.0)`) at radius 0, then 2 texels, the high-quality path (pixel shader 2.0, 8 stages) reduces it 4 × 4 to 64² and 16² (`Color*=(1.0/16.0)*.8`), and `DrawGlow` adds it over the frame: 8 taps of the 256 target (four axial ±0.00586/±0.0078, four diagonal ±0.0041/±0.0055), then 4 + 4 taps of the 64 and 16 targets at 2× and 3× the offsets, each through "Color/=6.0; Color-=.05".
93. **Cube images and bump** [Creature `DE_CEnvironment::ObjProcessReferences`; Direct3D `DE_CStandardRenderer::CubeSpecularEnvironmentImage` 0x20009ce0, `DE_CShader::CStage::SetStates` 0x20031b60]: `Image\CubeEnvironment` references chunk 3 lists its faces (Top, Front, Bottom, Left, Right, Back); a reflection stage can name its own cube (Desert's dunes: `ReflectionDunes`); a cube's or the environment's `HighRangeSpecular` child is the specular cube. A stage image with a `Bump` child image whose `BumpFactor` is not 0 bends the reflection: the bump pixel shader takes tex2D(Bump, UV) × factor with z = 0, rotates it world → camera, adds the normal and reflects; the fixed-function path sets BUMPENVMAT from the factor and a `RotateValue`.
94. **Stage colour values are constant** [Core `DE_CRenderMaterialElement::FlatColor` 0x7002f700, `DE_CValueInterFLOAT::GetValue(float&)` 0x7010cd40, `DE_CValueModulatedRGBA::GetValue(DE_CRGBA&)`]: a stage's colour Value is read through GetValue without a time, which evaluates inter values at 0 and a ModulatedRGBA's alpha at its time member (+100, 0 and not loaded), so the 58 stage colour values in the data are fixed colours.
95. **No fog**: no class, function or data object sets up distance fog; the only fog states are the glow layer's.

96. **Passive reference placement** [Core `DE_CPassiveActorReference::Create` 0x700929a0, `ObjPrepare` 0x70092980]: only mode 2 references create at ObjPrepare (vtable +0x164); mode 3 ones are created like the others (the data's mode 3 references are created when their branch is prepared here too). After the copy: +0x128 renames it, +0x124 deactivates it, the reference's own references are appended to it; modes 2/3 rename the reference, move its children to the created actor, `RefTargetReplace` every reference naming the reference onto it, deactivate the reference and `DeleteThisLater` it. Placement +0x11c 0: the created actor's transform is the reference's (+0x40, +0x4c) over its own and it is parented to the reference's parent (`ObjSetParent(ObjParent())`); otherwise the reference's cached world transform (+0x70, +0x7c) places it and it has no parent. Then, with +0x120 set, a reference in a scene adds the created actor to it (scene vtable +0x130) unless `DE_CActor::ShouldBeInnactive`. In the data every mode 2/3 reference has placement 0 (so +0x120 changes nothing for them); the script-created projectile references are placement 1, add 0 (their scripts `Scene().Add` the result); the Pickups and Pyros `ExplosionRef`s are placement 1, add 1.
97. **Music** [Core `SetMoodCaller` 0x70218b50 (CSpeaker +0xdc); AudioPlayer `DE_CDirectXAudioPlayer::ProcessPerformanceMessages`, `DE_CDirectMusicSegment`/`DE_CDirectMusicContainer`; scripts BMusicPlayer, BStandardOMFSession `AssureMusicBeingPlayed`, BLastManStanding]: `MusicPlayerActor()` is Music.DEObjects' `MusicPlayer` (a sphere affector with a `Speaker` whose noise is the theme, `theme_short`, and the BMusicPlayer behaviour: when its speaker is not playing it plays the queued noise, and a queued noise different from the playing one stops it). The session plays the scene's "Music" object reference (Desert: a song's `A-01`) once loading is complete, at mood Silent, then sets moods from the score and time progression (EMusicMood: 1 Silent, 2 Early, 3 Winning, 4 Tied, 5 Losing, 6 TimeWinning, 7 TimeTied, 8 TimeLosing, 9 WinningLopsided, 10 LosingLopsided). A `Noise2\DirectMusicSegment` = [v2: name, f32, f32][v1: u32 size, RIFF DMSG] with references chunk 2: [its DirectMusicContainer][its jumps: one segment per mood, the song's A..J columns]. On DMUS_NOTIFICATION_SEGALMOSTEND for a playing segment the player takes the segment's jump for the instance's mood (1..20), makes it the speaker's active noise and plays it. A `DirectMusicContainer` = [name][u32 size, RIFF DMCN] holding the song's DLS collection and style (DMST); the segments are style-based (tempo, time signature, command/groove, style reference and band tracks). The container's objects have no file names: a `LIST cosl` holds one `LIST cobl` per object ([`cobh`][RIFF DLS/DMST/DMBD]), named by UNFO UNAM (or the DLS's INFO INAM). The segments' DMRF references name them by file (`A-Silent.sty`, `!hexed DLS.dls`). ProcessPerformanceMessages acts on GUID_NOTIFICATION_SEGMENT with dwNotificationOption 2 (SEGALMOSTEND) only. A segment's DE_CNoise volume and frequency are 1.0 throughout the music files.

98. **Command manager** (ported in `natives_ai.cpp`) [Creature `DE_CCommandManager::Tick` 0x1501e000, `Execute` 0x1501de30, `Sort` 0x1501e380, `ReassignExecutors` 0x1501dd30, `ReceiveCommand`, `RemoveCommand`, `Forget`, `CControllerInfo::Activate`, `DE_CStandardHookupExecutor::Tick/Confidence`, `DE_CMoveToCommand::ModifySort/Update`; OMF `DE_CPilot::AttackBestEnemy` 0x3504d370, `Attack`]:
   - **Commands are objects.** Every `Command*` native creates a new command and appends it to the manager's list. It stays there until `CommandForget(mask)` removes the commands whose type lies entirely within the mask. Types: navigation 2, look-at 4, attack 0x10, aquire 0x20. Every command has priority 1 (`+0x48`). So `AttackBestEnemy`, called every frame, piles up attack commands that the pilot only clears when it changes state.
   - **Sort** runs only when the list has changed. Each command's sort key starts at its priority. `MoveTo::ModifySort` sets the key to 0 while the creature is within the required distance of the command's current move-to position. The list is then bubble-sorted by key, highest first, keeping insertion order on ties.
   - **Reassign.** With the type mask 0x3e, the first sorted command is executed, then each later command whose type has not been taken yet. So only the first command of each type takes part, and for attack commands that is the oldest one.
   - **Execute** picks the controller's executor with the highest confidence. Attack and aquire commands score their value (`+0x78`: aggression or urgency); move and look score 1. A command whose confidence is strictly greater than the current primary's becomes the primary, and the old primary moves to the backup list. Otherwise the command is added as a backup, unless its confidence is 0, in which case it is dropped.
   - **Tick** (once per creature tick):
     - Every command runs `Update`; a move-to command takes its next navigation-path position.
     - The primary runs. If it acted (did not call `ExecutorDidNothing`) and is an attack or aquire command, nothing else runs.
     - Otherwise every backup runs in list order.
     - A controller info's counter (`+0x3c`, never reset) goes up when the primary did nothing (above 8: reassign) or when there is no primary (above 20: reassign).
     - Masks: `+0x90` 0x3e, `+0x98` 6, `+0x9c` 0x30.
   - **Hookup executor:** `ExecutorDidNothing` clears `+0x30`, which `Tick` sets before each hook call. `DeltaTick` is never registered for this object (only Core calls `AddDeltaTick`).
   - **AttackBestEnemy** walks the creature's known needs (`+0x168`), taking each need's owner:
     - The score is distance / (need desire(4) + 0.01). The current target's distance is scaled down by its age, as already ported.
     - An owner in the pilot's Love list (`+0x348`) has its distance multiplied by Valor (`+0x7a8`) × 2. One in the Hate list (`+0x35c`) has it divided by Anger (`+0x768`) × 2.
     - Owners with `GlobalInterest` ≤ 0 are skipped.
     - Only single-player sub-sessions fill the lists (`AddIfAbsentHate`).
