   - **Attack** changing the target sets the quality-degradation offset (`+0x3d0`) to −(q − 0.5) when q > 0.5, else 0. `NormalizedAIQualityDegredation` returns q + offset. For a user-controlled target the offset becomes 0 when a master-session member's vtable slot 0x154 returns 0; I couldn't identify that method.

99. **Path animation** [Core `DE_CAnimationStreamPlayer::ObjProcessContents` 0x701c1cb0, `SetTime` 0x701c1e30, `SetTimeAndPointActor` 0x701c20a0, `GetTimeForDistance` 0x701c2310; `DE_CActor::PointZ` 0x700911d0]:
   - **Data.** An `AnimationStreamPlayer` is v1: [name][DE_CStream v1: i32 n {f32 t, x, y, z}; i32 n {f32 t, quaternion}]. This is the same stream as in animation banks.
   - **No looping.** The loop flag (`+0x48`) is cleared by `ObjInitContents` and set by nothing, so times are never wrapped. Scripts wrap the time themselves (`BRenderPathFollower`: scene time / `PathTicksPerLoop`, fractional part, + `PathOffset`).
   - **Key search.** Start at keys 0/1 (1 when t ≥ key 0's time), then advance both while t is beyond the later key. Before the first key, or with a single key, the first key's value is used. Otherwise interpolate with factor (t − a.t) / (b.t − a.t), which is not clamped, so it extrapolates past the last key.
   - **SetTime** sets the actor's local orientation (slerp) and local position (linear).
   - **SetTimeAndPointActor** additionally calls `PointZ` toward the later key's position whenever t is before that key. `PointZ` builds a frame whose Z axis points from the actor's world position to the given position (the key's path-space position, used as a world position), then makes it local to the parent.
   - **GetTimeForDistance(d, t)** handles only negative d: it walks back segment by segment from t and returns 0 past the start. It returns 1 for any other d.
100. **Fighter against fighter** (ported in `forces.cpp`) [OMF `DE_CFighterPhysics::Tick` 0x35039970 (the pass at 0x35039be6..0x35039f09), `ObjPrepare` 0x35039730; `DE_COMF2Fighter::ObjPrepare` 0x35025010; Core `DE_CSphereAffector::IsTouchingSphere` 0x70089c30, `DE_TCoord3D::AdjustMomentumToSurfaceNormal`]:
   - **Spheres.** Each fighter needs a child sphere affector named `OtherFighterPushSphere` (physics +0x1b0; an error otherwise). Every shipped robot's has a falloff of 5.5 and sits 5.5 above the fighter's origin. The results it collects (+0x1b4) keep depth (+0x1d0), normal (+0x1d4) and point (+0x1dc), and are cleared each tick.
   - **Who.** The other fighters in the scene (+0x334, filled pairwise by `DE_COMF2Fighter::ObjPrepare`), walked from the last. A fighter whose `IsAlive` (vtable +0x17c) is false, or whose own skip flag (+0x188, `SkipFighterCollision`) is set, is passed. When this fighter's skip flag is set, the whole pass is skipped and the flag is cleared.
   - **Test.** The two sphere centres must be under 120 apart. This fighter's sphere is then moved vertically towards the other's for the test, by up to the push size (+0x50c) less its diameter: the other's push size when this one is higher, its own when lower, never past the other's height. The spheres touch when the distance is under the sum of their outer radii (+0x17c each). The depth is that sum less the distance, and the normal points from this sphere to the other.
   - **Push.** The fighter's local position moves back along the normal by min(depth, distance moved since the last tick + 0.1). If the momentum goes into the other (momentum · normal > 0), 5% of its normal part is removed (AdjustMomentumToSurfaceNormal leaves the tangential part).
   - **Not called.** `PushAgainstOtherFighter` and `ClampMomentumAgainstOtherFighter` are the inlined copies; nothing calls them.
   - **In-fight natives decoded with it.**
     - **Box corners.** `CBoxAffector::Set{Lower,Upper}{X,Y,Z}` store the box corners (+0x188.., +0x194..). Katana's projectile grows its hit box this way.
     - **Projectile owner.** `SetAsProjectileCreator` sets a projectile's creator (+0x314) and owner (+0x308). Only state packing reads the creator.
     - **Copied placement.** `CopyWorldToLocal` copies a target's world placement into this actor's local placement. Jaguar's laser starts at its `TargetingSight`.
     - **Scene ownership.** `OwnBranch` is `DE_CScene::OwnTree`, which only keeps references for lifetime.
     - **Enemies.** `IsEnemy` is decided by the caller's class: `DE_CCreature` 0; `DE_CPilot` asks its creature (+0x320); `DE_COMF2Fighter` treats everything but itself as an enemy, except a fighter of the same team (+0x49c ≥ 0).
     - **Lean.** `AnimTurn(action)` eases the lean (+0x450) towards ±walk max (+0x530, actions 2/3) or ±run max (+0x538, actions 12/13), else towards 0, by +0x53c per call, and sets +0x458 = RotateY(lean°). `ValidateAnimationTime` turns the Pelvis (+0x43c) by it and resets it; `AnimReset` clears both.
     - **Surface feedback.** `ApplySurfaceCollisionForce(k)` gives each touched surface the fighter's force once, at 100·k·(momentum · normal) + 3 (3 when still).
     - **Vacuum momentum.** `SetupTransferedMomentumForVacuum` scales the creator-to-victim direction (creator's frame, normalised) per axis, reverses it and turns it back to world.
     - **Other stores.** `SetIsRestingOnFloorOff` clears the floor flag (+0x18c). `CRandomEllipsoidSpawner::SetRadius` sets all three radii and rebuilds the box around its centre. `CBillboardedQuadParticleRenderer::SetColor` sets the renderer's colour value (+0x58).
101. **AVI images** (`de/avi.h`) [Core `DE_CAVIImage::ObjProcessContents` 0x7018ac10, `SetPhase` 0x7018a550, `GetChannelData` 0x7018adc0, `DE_CAVI::ProcessContents`; `DE_CImage::SetPhase` 0x7018e670]:
   - **Data.** An `Image\AVI` is [DE_CImage chunk][v2 chunk [DE_CAVI v1 chunk: BOOL present, DE_CDataBlock = a RIFF AVI file]]. Its size comes from the video, and its pixels are the frame's RGB (no alpha).
   - **Phase.** `SetPhase` (vtable +0x16c) stores the phase (+0x54) and marks the image changed. The AVI image shows frame trunc(phase × frames) for 0 ≤ phase < 1, frame 0 otherwise; loading sets phase 0.
   - **Codecs.** The 36 shipped videos are 24-bit DIB (bottom-up BGR), Cinepak, Indeo 4.1 (`iv41`) or Indeo 5 (`iv50`). Materials reach them as a texture manager's image (alongside `Image\Standard`).
102. **Robot-specific natives** (found by running every robot against another, in `--direct` and session mode):
   - **Body rotation.** `AnimRotate{X,Y,Z}Deg` / `AnimRotate{X,Y,Z}` (OMF 0x350208d0.., 0x35020960..), `AnimTurn(action)` and `AnimRotateTo` all turn one per-frame coordinate system (+0x458) about the fighter's own axes; `ValidateAnimationTime` (0x35026110) turns the Pelvis by it and resets it. Nothing keeps a rotation from one frame to the next. Before this, `AnimRotateYDeg` was stored as a lasting yaw.
   - **Moves.** `CActor::Move(x,y,z)` / `Move(CVector)` (Core 0x7008d220 / 0x7008d1c0) add to the local position as given, in the parent's frame. Our `Move(CVector)` had been converting from world.
   - **Local turns.** `CActor::RotateLocal{X,Y,Z}` (0x7008dbd0..) are the degree forms in radians.
   - **Coordinate systems.** `CCoordinateSystem::Set(cs)` copies the other's nine floats, or resets to identity when given none.
   - **Momentum.** `CPhysics::GetMomentum(v)` is physics vtable +0x124. `COMF2Fighter::DampenVerticalMomentum(k)` (0x35022520) scales y by 1 − |y|·k, stopping at 0. `Decrease/IncreaseGravityMultiplierTo` (0x35021640 / 0x35021680) lower or raise the multiplier (+0x19c), never the other way.
   - **Controller direction.** `Get2DOrientation` is controller vtable +0x130; `Is2DOrientationRight/Left` ask for exactly 2 / 6.
   - **Attack flags.** `SetNoEvade` sets flag 0x10 (+0xc4). The `AddAttackType*` / `IsAttackType*` bits at +0x4c are Standard 2, Projectile 4, Cold 8, Heat 0x10, Electrical 0x20, Gravitational 0x40, TimeStop 0x80 and NoReplicate 0x80000000; `ClearAttackType` zeroes them. 2.112 puts Light at 4 and moves the rest up one bit (FINDINGS 214).
   - **Hit streams.** `CloseHitStream(n)` (0x350168a0) gives every fighter victim the attack's hit names 0..n−1.
   - **Registration.** `RegisterStandardComboAttack(frames, power)` is `GuideToLastActorHit` + `RegisterStandardState`.
   - **Scene tests.** The scene keeps a test sphere (+0x150) and a test cone (+0x154).
     - `TestSphere` places the sphere and sets its falloff, then calls `IsTouching(results)`.
     - `TestSpherePath` (0x70072f80) steps it radius/2 at a time from one step out, backwards for a negative distance, and stops at the first touch.
     - `TestCone` sets the cone's position, orientation, penumbra and far end.
     - `TestLineSegment` is that cone with penumbra 0.
   - **Cone against sphere.** `DE_CConeAffector::IsTouchingSphere` (0x70080560) needs three things:
     - the sphere within far end + r of the apex;
     - its depth along +Z, ± r, overlapping near end .. far end;
     - its distance from the axis under |z|·tan(penumbra) + (1 + tan)·r.
   - **Collision results.**
     - `AddTestAffectors` appends the other tester's affectors.
     - `CCollisionEvent::CollisionResults` is +0x44.
     - `GetAverageSurfaceNormal` sums the touched surfaces' own normals (+0x18) and normalises them.
     - `SetupArenaSurfaceHit` makes an OMF2 event of type 0xb with the results (+0x44) and power (+0x48). Nothing in the DLLs or scripts handles type 0xb.
   - **Vectors.**
     - `ReflectBySurfaceNormal(n)` gives d − 2(d·n)n at the same length.
     - `Reverse` negates.
     - `LocalTimeScale` is `SceneTicksPerUpdate` (+0x1dc) as a float.
   - **Components.** `CCreature::AddComponent` (Creature 0x15017180) makes the creature a component (+0x218, list +0x128) and child. It aligns the child's creation time (+0x1d8) to ours, rounded to the child's update interval, so that the two update in step. `SetInstanceNumber` appends digits to the ID suffix that only `BuildID` (replication) reads.
   - **Images and meshes.**
     - `CImage::AddFilter` appends to the image's filter list (+0x34): Mantis's bug images get the fighter's paint filter.
     - `Enable/DisableAllMeshProperties` enable or disable every mesh property for the actor (vtable +0x150/+0x154) and switch its particle instances' spawning (+0x58).
     - `SetBranchUnrendered` stops rendering the whole tree.
     - `AddCopyToScene(p)` adds a copy of p, placed by this actor's world transform, to the scene.
   - **Events and paths.** `SetAsRegisteredEvent(name)` gives the event the handle registered under that name. `Path()` is `ObjPath`: the names from the root down, with a separator not decoded.
   - **Late additions.** These turned up once more code paths ran:
     - `CValueFLOAT::GetNormalizedLoopingValue(t)` (0x7010f2e0) is the value at t − trunc(t).
     - `CCoordinateSystem::PointZ(v)` (0x7014b450) is `Set(normalised v)`.
     - `CSimplePhysics::SetAcceleration(x,y,z)` stores the acceleration (+0x98).
     - `CCollisionResults::AddGroupsToTest` ORs into the groups (+0x34).
     - `CCreature::InvalidateOrientation` only marks cached transforms stale.
     - `CActor::PointZByRotationDeg(target, x, y, z)` (0x70091380): Canyon's tracking guns turn with it. It takes the target's direction in the actor's world frame as X/Y/Z angles (`Set` then `Decompose` 0x70027230), clamps each to ±its limit (a 0 limit gives 0), and turns the local orientation about the parent's X, Y, then Z. Despite the name, the limits are compared with the angles in radians.
     - `COMF2Fighter::NewGetHitSpark(pos, angle)` (0x35026a60): the hit-spark copy (FINDINGS 103) at a position, oriented `Set(angle)`, with power |angle|. It returns the copy; arena hazards use it.
   - **Natives the DLLs don't register.** Eleven native keys the scripts use (ten names) have neither a `Caller` nor their name in any module DLL (`OPENBG_LIST_NATIVES` against the dumps); (superseded by FINDINGS 123: these were our registrations, not calls the shipped bytecode makes, and none is the exe's). Of those, `IsCreatureActive` (slot +0x30 ≠ 0) and `RegisterUrgentState` (empty) still have their methods in Session.ModuleDLL.
103. **Hit sparks** [OMF `DE_COMF2Fighter::NewGetHitEffect` 0x35026710 (vtable +0x23c), `DE_CSparks` (`Mesh\Sparks`) `SetupSparks` 0x35042f10 / `DE_CInstanceInfo::Process` 0x350433e0, `DE_CSparkProcess::Tick` 0x3502f890]:
   - **Copy.** Unless the effect type (+0x48) is 0 or 4, the fighter's child "SparkEffect" is copied (`NewCopy(1)`), placed at the connect point (+0x7c) and added to the scene with a `DE_CSparkProcess`. The child is a deactivated template that the fighter's `SparkEffectReference` creates.
   - **Orientation.** Effect type 1 sets the copy's orientation from the effect direction (+0x88), using `DE_CCoordinateSystem::Set(v)` 0x70025fe0: Z = v, Y = world up made perpendicular, X = Y × Z. Type 3 takes X from the second direction (+0x94) and Z from the first, sets Y = X × Z, and switches the sparks to the spray.
   - **Amount.** With m = |effect direction|:
     - the Sparks child gets 11 − trunc(−4m) sparks when m > 1, none otherwise;
     - the light child is deleted when m < 3, else coloured (1, 0.7, 0.3) at min(1.5m, 1).
   - **Spawn.** Sparks spawn once, on the first draw, at the actor ± 1.5 on each axis.
     - Decay is U·0.003145 + 0.000786 per ms and width U·0.2 + 0.16.
     - Type 0 is a fan along the actor's Z: the position moves up to 7.5 along it, and the velocity is Z·(1−U)·0.10752 plus a jitter of ±0.057·U.
     - Type 1 is a spray in the actor's frame.
     - Both then move 80 ms along their velocity.
   - **Each draw**, with dt the scene ms since the last (at most 1000):
     - position += velocity·dt;
     - life −= dt·decay;
     - velocity.y −= dt·3.84e-5.
   - **Shape.** Each live spark is a view-space kite from its head p to its tail p − velocity·220. The tip is `width` past the head, the back `width` towards it, and the sides `width` across the screen.
   - **Colour.** (1, life, 0.75·life) at alpha 1 − (1 − life)². It is drawn with the Sparks mesh's first image, material property 8 (the particle renderers' additive premultiplied blend).
   - **Process.** The process loses 0.005 of life a tick and deletes the copy after 200 ticks. Its light loses 0.05 a tick.
   - **Pack/Unpack.** `PackForGetHitEffect` (0x35016e00) packs effect, reaction and attack type, the three vectors and the flag byte, with the damage as the byte trunc(clamp(d, 0, 50)·255). `UnpackForGetHitEffect` (0x35016f30) restores them but discards that byte: it sets the damage to clamp(own damage, 0, 50)/255·50. 2.123 uses the byte (FINDINGS 214).
104. **Secondary rigs and sequence managers** (Gargoyle's wings):
   - **Separate rig.** A fighter can carry a second rig: another `ActorDeformInfo` (`WingDeformInfo`) weighting a second model mesh (`DE_Wings Mesh`), animated by another `AnimationSequenceGroup` (`WingAnimationBank`).
   - **Manager.** That bank is driven by a separate `AnimationSequenceManager` (`WingAnimationManager`, under `WingBase`, which hangs from `Chest`). `BWings` drives it with `ActivatePrelude`/`SetTime`/`Activate`.
   - **Sequence handles.** `BWings`'s `seqWingsCurledUp` resolves against the bank named among its behavior's globals, since its owner is not a creature.
   - **Bind space.** The wing binds are in model space, at Chest + the tree offsets.
105. **Cone light projectors, fully decoded** [Direct3D `DE_CStandardProjectorShader::SetupConeProjector` 0x20034540 / 0x20034750, `DrawPatches` 0x20033b20, `SetStates` 0x20033830, `DE_CShader::CStage::SetStates`, `DE_CD3DLight::UpdateConeUVWs`, `DE_CVertexShader::StackConeLightProjection` 0x20037410]:
   - **Image.** The renderer's shared cone projector uses the core `LightMap` (`GlobalLightMap`). A light with its own image (Light references v3: BOOL + reference, kept at +0x30) gets a projector of its own with that image.
   - **Stage 0.** COLOROP MODULATE(TEXTURE, TFACTOR), ALPHAOP SELECTARG1, clamp. Coordinates are texcoord set 0 under D3DTTFF_COUNT3 | PROJECTED with an identity matrix. The patches' FVF is 0x10102 (XYZ, one 3D texcoord).
   - **CPU projection.** `UpdateConeUVWs` fills that texcoord on the CPU. Each touched vertex goes into the light's frame as l, and gets (l.x + w/2, l.y + w/2, w) with w = −2·tan(penumbra)·l.z. Projected, this maps the penumbra cone onto the image.
   - **Dead shader.** The vertex-shader version of the same projection (`vs.1.0` built from text: m4x4 r2, v0, c0 / mov oPos, r2 / m4x4 r0, v0, c4 / rcp r1, r0.z / mul r0.xy by r1.w / mov oT0.xyz, r0.xyz) is exported but never called.
   - **Stage 1.** MODULATE(TEXTURE, CURRENT) with the core `ShadowAttenuation` image: 32×32, row 0 black, then 234 falling linearly to about 0 down the rows, constant across. Its coordinates are the camera-space position (D3DTSS_TCI_CAMERASPACEPOSITION, COUNT3). Render actors are camera-relative (the renderer's +0x130/+0x13c are the camera's position and rows).
   - **Stage-1 matrix.** The inverse of [−d·X; −d·Y; −d·Z; origin], times RotationX(90°). The origin is the light position + Z·near-begin (+0x184), and d is the far end (+0x180) − near-begin. So (u, v) = (−x/d, (z − near)/d), clamped: black behind the near plane, fading out at the far end.
   - **States.** Blend ONE/ONE for modes 0 and 1. No lighting, no z writes, LESSEQUAL, CULL_CW, and a small depth bias.
106. **Skeleton validation, edge probe and the computer opponents' decisions** (slices 1 and 2):
   - **Pose validation.** `DE_CCreature` +0x1c8 marks the bone actors as showing the current animation time.
     - `AnimSetTimeCaller` stores the time (+0x1c4), clears the flag and calls `ValidateAnimationTime` (vtable +0x184) at once.
     - The prelude and capture callers validate first.
     - `InternalWorldUpdate` validates a stale pose when something needs world transforms.
     - The fighter's override (0x35026110) turns the Pelvis (+0x43c, orientation and position) by the accumulated body rotation (+0x458), then resets it. So `AnimRotate*` and the lean move the real skeleton, with its hit spheres, anchors and focal point, from that `AnimSetTime` until the next one.
     - The follows (`AnimHeadFollow` 0x35026310 and the centre-of-gravity follow) move only the Pelvis actor, from whatever the skeleton currently is. The renderer now draws the bone actors as they stand.
   - **Edge probe.** In `DE_CFighterPhysics::Tick` (0x35039970), a standing fighter that lost the floor is put 2 units below its moved position, the one before this tick's collision and fighter pushes, and probes. Without a floor it returns to the pushed position and collides again. Walking down a slope triggers it most frames, since a grounded fighter has no gravity.
   - **Missing targets.** In `DE_CStandardHookupExecutor::Tick` (Creature 0x1502b650), an attack command whose targets are gone attacks the creature's `Enemy(0)` (vtable +0x16c). An aquire command without targets calls no hook and counts as acted.
   - **Enemies.** `DE_COMF2Fighter::NumEnemies` / `Enemy` (0x3502a3a0 / 0x3502a3e0) list the linked fighters (+0x334) that count this fighter as an enemy. `IsEnemyFighter` (0x3502a740) means not itself and not the same team, with +0x49c ≥ 0. The team comes from the pilot's faction (+0x32c → vtable +0x180).
   - **Global interest.** `GlobalInterest` (0x3502ae40) is recomputed only when more than 300 ms of scene time have passed since the last time (+0x488). It is the fighter's own interest (+500) × (900 − d + 1) for each enemy within 900. The cache (+0x48c) starts at 1, so `IsAlive` lags a death by up to 300 ms.
   - **Quality offset.** In `DE_CPilot::Attack` (0x3504d280), against a user-controlled target the quality offset is cleared unless the master session's +0x38 reports `IsJoinable` (vtable +0x154, the slot `IsJoinableCaller` reads). Only a joinable network game keeps the computer's handicap against a human.
   - **Needs.** `InternalUpdateNeedInfo` (0x15018400) runs the scene's `TestSphere` at the creature's `FocalPoint` (vtable +0x154), radius 100000, groups 0x800. It takes each touched affector's Need property (`FindPropertyByType`), walking the results from the last. A need held by a plain creature, like the energy boost's "New OMFNeed", is never known.
   - **Result order.** `DE_CAffector::IsTouching` (0x7007b900) walks the root scene's affector list in order and does not sort. `SortByTouchDistance` is a bubble sort on the touch points' distance that swaps only for a gain above 0.005.
107. **What lights light, and detail levels** [Direct3D `DE_CStandardRenderLight::UpdateLitActors` 0x2000f720, `DE_CStandardRenderAffector::SetAffector`; Core `DE_CActor::ObjProcessContents` 0x7008fca0, `ObjInitContents`, `ObjPrepare`, `UpdateForDetailLevel`, `ShouldBeInnactive` 0x70091040, `IsPositionStatic` 0x700917c0]:
   - **Lit actors.** A light's affector runs `IsTouching` against collision group 0x20. Each touched affector's render affector is lit when two things hold:
     - its light mask (+0x114) shares a bit with the light's (+100);
     - the light's affector is not position-static, or the render affector's actor is not (+0x10c = `IsPositionStatic` of that actor).
   - **Static lights.** A static light never lights static geometry. Every arena root, and the light parents under it, are position-static (mode 1). So the arena's spotlights light only what moves (robots, trains, BladePit's blades, mode 2) and leave the arena to its baked look. Before this, City's 32 cones and BladePit's six washed the floors out.
   - **Position mode.** Actor contents v4+ carry it after the four BOOLs (+0x10c: 0 inherit, 1 static, 2 dynamic), and v5+ add the flags (+0x110). The defaults (`ObjInitContents`) are mode 2 and flags 6. `IsPositionStatic` takes the nearest set mode up the tree, and is false when none is set.
   - **Detail levels.** `ObjPrepare` flags an actor 0x20, and `UpdateForDetailLevel` leaves it out of the scene, when its flags lack the current level's bit: 4 at high detail, 2 at low. The level is the registry BOOL read into 0x7035dec0, default 1 (high).
     - FirePit's `LowDetailFloor` (flags 0x1a) is therefore absent at high detail. We had been drawing it over the glowing grating.
     - `IsHighDetailGeometry` returns that level, and `ShouldBeInactive` is the same flag test.
108. **Arena behaviour** (slice 3):
   - **Clock.** `DE_CWindow::Run` (Core) updates and draws a frame once at least 20 ms have passed (`RunMaximumCPU` lifts the cap), so frames come at 50 a second at most. The scene clock is real milliseconds.
   - **Creature updates.** `DE_CCreature::SetTime` (Creature 0x15015d20) runs a creature at creation + n × `SceneTicksPerUpdate`, catching up at most 100 updates.
     - The creation time is the tick of its rate's temporal set: `DE_CScene::AddTemporal` / `TemporalElementSetForRate` start each set at (time / rate) × rate. So every creature of a rate updates together, on multiples of it.
     - With actor flag 0x10 the creation time is 1.
     - A fighter's rate is 16 ms, so fighters update 62.5 times a second. The port's world tick is now one 16 ms step, where it had been a 60 Hz frame (4% slow).
   - **Line test.** `DE_CScene::TestLineSegment` (0x700731b0) uses the scene's test cone at the start point, pointed at the end, with penumbra 0 and far end the length, then asks it `IsTouching(results)` with the results' own groups. Scripts read `Collision(0)`'s surface normal, for example Katana's projectile bouncing.
   - **Cone against sphere.** With penumbra 0, the cone touches a sphere whose centre is within r of the axis and at depth −r .. length + r. The touch point is on the axis.
   - **Scene removal.** `RemoveActorCaller` takes the actor out of the scene (scene vtable +0x134, the counterpart of +0x130 `Add`).
   - **Image swaps.** `RemoveAllProperty` clears a texture manager's or mesh's properties, and `AddProperty` of an image makes it what that texture manager draws. Canyon's gun shows its shooter's pilot photo this way, and pilots their mood icon, each re-adding the actor to the scene to rebuild its mesh.
   - **Attach.** `DE_CActor::Attach` (0x70091c30) parents the object to the actor, keeping its world offset in the actor's frame. `DE_COMF2Fighter::Attach` (0x3502c0e0) parents it to the Pelvis at the Pelvis's origin.
   - **Owner.** `DE_CUniqueActorProperty::Owner` (0x700937f0) is the property's actor, or that affector's owner (+0x118).
   - **Intensity values.** Every intensity value in the shipped arenas is a box's `Value\FLOAT\ImageMap` (the danger maps); sphere and cone affectors carry none.
109. **Landings, and what `--direct` was missing**:
   - **Floor events.** A fighter's `TouchFloor`/`LeaveFloor` handlers run only from `DE_CFighterPhysics::Tick`'s surface contacts. `BFighter`'s setup registers `.TouchFloor` once (`AddTouchFloorRecipient(exThis(), .TouchFloor)`), and the current state's override handles it. `StandardGetHit.Flying.HeadFirstFaceUp` loops until then (`Time++` keeps its loop going); its `TouchFloor` bounces or calls `Ground.GetUpFaceUp`.
   - **The bug.** `--direct` never created the arena environment, so there were no surface affectors in the fighters' group (0x200000). Fighters fell back to a flat height field that raises no floor events. Jumps still landed (`AirFall` polls the floor), but a thrown fighter never got up. `--direct` now creates the environment as the session's picker does: the description's resource, entered into the scene.
   - **Spawn points.** `BStandardOMFSession::GatherAllSpawnPoints` takes the scene's actors with a `BPlayerSpawnPoint` behaviour, sorted by name, and slot n spawns at point n. `--direct` now does the same; Volcano's name-matched point 1 had been 5000 units up.
   - **Overhead throw, end to end.** Jaguar jumps and presses both punches 10 units from a still Katana in Desert. `AirOverheadThrow` → `OverheadThrowHit`; Katana goes `AirGetThrown` (dangling head grab, following `ThrowFollowTarget`) → `StandardGetHit.FlyingHeadFirstFaceUp` (−15%) → `FlyingBounce` → `FlyingFall` → `GroundGetUpFaceUp` → standing. The "Unknown Throw Reaction Type" log is the original script's: `ThrowableSetReactionAnimation` has no branch for `reactSwingingHeadGrab`.
110. **Light projectors and reach, finished** [Direct3D `DE_CD3DLight::UpdateTouchedPolygons` 0x2002cd00, `SetupComplete` 0x2002d1d0, `DE_CStandardProjectorShader::SetStates` 0x20033830 / `SetupConeProjector(DE_CLight)` 0x20034750, `DE_CGenericShader::ActivateAndDrawPatches`, `DE_CD3DRenderMeshElement::InitialSetup`, `DE_CStandardRenderAffector::SetAffector`, `DE_CStandardRenderer::FindRenderActor`; Core `DE_CLight::ObjProcessReferences` 0x701bb180, `ObjInitContents`]:
   - **No facing test.** `UpdateTouchedPolygons` takes, for each mesh element whose render affector the light lights, the polygons in octree cells the light's affector touches (`GetPotentialCollisions`, or a sphere query for type 7). The pass has lighting off, blending on, `LESSEQUAL` without depth writes, and `D3DCULL_CW`. So a polygon facing away from the light is lit if the camera sees it.
   - **Culling.** Every generic shader pass culls `D3DCULL_CW` (render state 0x16 = 2), unless the shader has flag 0x10, which comes from mesh flag 0x4000 (two-sided). In GL that is back-face culling with counter-clockwise front faces; the other way loses Desert's floor and sky.
   - **A light's own image.** Light references v3 add [BOOL][reference] → +0x2c/+0x30. `SetupComplete`:
     - a cone (type 1) without one joins the shared cone projector (`GlobalLightMap`, ONE/ONE);
     - a cone with an Image gets its own projector over that image;
     - a sphere (type 7) without one joins the shared sphere projector, and one with an image gets none.
   - **Blend.** The blend comes from the mode (+0x50: 0/1 → 0, 2 → 2, 3 → 1), overridden by +0x54 (1 → 3, 2 → 4, 3 → 5). `SetStates` maps 0 and 3 to ONE/ONE, 1 and 5 to SRCALPHA/INVSRCALPHA (the stages then carry alpha), 2 to ZERO/INVSRCCOLOR, and 4 to ONE/INVSRCCOLOR. All shipped image lights are mode 3, which reaches mask 8:
     - scorch and splat decals (Jaguar, Pyros, Warlord `LargeScorch`, the nuke's `NukeScorch`, Mantis `AcidSplat`, alpha-blended);
     - hot marks, a lightning hit, and the pilots' current-target marker (`CurrentTargetProjectedImage`), additive.
   - **Masks.** `InitialSetup` gives a vertex-lit element (+0x160 bit 1) mask 2, and a projector-lit one (bit 2) masks 4, 8 and 0x10. So decals and darkening lights reach the same surfaces as splash lights.
   - **Reach.** `SetAffector` joins a render affector to the render actor of its owner (+0x118, else itself) when its groups have 0x20 (0x10 makes it a visibility affector). A light lights what those owners draw.
     - Fighters own their `EncompasingAffector` (on the Pelvis), and arenas their visibility sphere.
     - City's car and BladePit's blades own their own spheres.
     - BladePit's surface `Encompaser`s own themselves and so light nothing.
     - A mesh takes the nearest owner up its tree. Pickups and projectiles have none and take no scene lights.
111. **The current target** [OMF `DE_COMF2Fighter::CurrentTarget` 0x3502a8b0, `ObjPrepare` 0x35025010]:
   - **When.** It is reconsidered when 1000 ms of scene time have passed since the last look. A look that keeps the same target sets the clock back 800 ms, so the next one comes after 200.
   - **Candidates.** Every other fighter in the scene, in the list `ObjPrepare` builds both ways (+0x334), walked from the last.
   - **Score.**
     - Base: 100 / (d + 100), plus the forward part of the unit direction in this fighter's frame when that is positive.
     - The current pick (+0x30c) × (2000 / ms since it was chosen + 1).
     - × `GlobalInterest` (vtable +0x158) when that is under 1.
     - × 0.25 for a non-enemy (`IsEnemyFighter`, +0x254).
   - **Effect.** The HUD reads it for the target name, photo and bars, and to place the target marker, so the HUD said "No Target" while we used the melee target. Fighter scripts read it too: propel distances and `Ground.GetUpFaceUp`'s guide.
112. **Factions, the environment GUID and the observer camera's natives**:
   - **Factions.** `FactionCaller`/`SetFactionCaller` (OMF 0x350482e0) give a pilot's faction reference (+0x328, pointer +0x32c). Only `BCreatureAndPilotPicker` sets it, and only in a team game (`StandardSessionIsTeamGame`). In Last Man Standing every pilot's faction is null, the HUD takes its non-team layout, and `Faction().Photo()` asks a null object.
     - `DE_CFaction` is a creature whose references end in a v1 chunk [BOOL][photo, +0x30c][RGBA advanced reference, +0x314].
     - `TeamIndex` is faction vtable +0x180; only the Sport scripts read it.
   - **Environment GUID.** `SetEnvironmentGUIDCaller` (Session 0x4500e680) copies the GUID to the session's description (+0x38 → +0xac), and `EnvironmentGUIDCaller` reads it back.
   - **Controllers.** `CreatureControllerCaller` / `NumCreatureControllerCaller` (Creature 0x15013620) read the creature's controller list (+0x13c, count +0x140), its `CreatureController\...` children. The observer camera has `GamepadController` (JoystickInputManager) and `MouseAxisController` (MouseAxis).
   - **Analog axes.** `RetrieveAndClearAnalogAxisMovement` (0x150019a0) returns and clears the axis's stored movement (+0x90). A mouse controller bound to a device (+0x2c ≠ −1) reads the mouse instead (0x15028260).
   - **Physics DeltaTick.** `DE_CSimplePhysics::DeltaTick` (Core 0x701dc060) ignores its argument. It steps over the scene time since its last run:
     - momentum += acceleration × Δ, then a move by momentum × Δ;
     - gravity × 0.0001445 per ms (`Tick` uses 0.037 per tick);
     - then `PerformCollisionTests`.
   - **Creature DeltaTick.** `DE_CCreature::DeltaTick` (0x15016700) runs once per scene time:
     - it restores a replayed state;
     - it ticks the command manager (+0x118) unless the scene is paused;
     - it calls the surface-change hooks.
113. **The shipped videos** (`deobj-dump avis <Data> <dir>` writes them out as `.avi` files):
   - **Count.** 36 `Image\AVI` objects:
     - 11 uncompressed 24-bit DIBs and 3 Cinepak;
     - 20 Indeo 5 (`IV50`): force fields, water, holograms; 16 distinct files;
     - 2 Indeo 4 (`IV41`): Desert's energy waves;
     - Jaguar's "New AVIImage", which holds no video.
   - **Indeo 4.** Both files are all intra frames: one luma and one chroma band, slant transforms, half-pel. No transparency, Haar transform or B-frames.
   - **Indeo 5.** Single-band pictures, intra and inter frames, 4×4 chroma blocks.
   - **Decoding.** openindeo decodes all of them bit-exactly against FFmpeg's decoder.
   - **Padding chunk.** City's two `WaterFlow` videos carry a 101st chunk in the padding after the RIFF data. The stream header (and so `SetPhase`) counts 100 frames; FFmpeg's index-based reader picks the extra one up.
114. **The remaining particle modifiers and renderers** [Particles module]:
   - **Rotator** (0x40008f10 / Modify 0x40009150). v1: [mode][FLOAT curve][factor]. It works on the rotation channel (`Setup` enables channel 5):
     - mode 1: += curve(age) × factor × dt;
     - mode 3: the same × size;
     - mode 4: = curve(age) + factor.
   - **OriginMagnet** (0x40008210). A point moves from a particle's spawn position to the actor as the particle ages (actor + (spawn − actor) × (1 − age)). The particle is drawn towards that point by its remaining life.
   - **PushDownZModifier** (0x400088d0).
     - v2: [amount, default 20][mode]; v1 means mode 5.
     - Each step a particle moves along the actor's X / Y / Z by amount × its age step.
     - Modes 3-5 also multiply by 1 − age.
   - **MiscellaneousNewParticleFunctions** (0x40004980). A new particle's orientation turns about its own X / Y / Z by a random angle within ± half the curve at its age, in degrees.
   - **BeamSpawner** (0x4000a460).
     - Once per instance it lays particles along the actor's local Z, from the start distance (`SetInitialSpawnLength`, +100) to the length (`SetTotalLength`, +0x60).
     - The spacing is +0x68 plus up to +0x6c; each particle sits on a ring of radius +0x70 plus up to +0x74 at a random angle.
     - Its `CommonSpawnerNewParticles` places nothing.
     - Jaguar's laser sets the length from its collision tests (`BLaser::Pulse`).
   - **LighteningSpawner** (0x4000bf40, `Bolt` 0x4000bd40).
     - Each spawned particle sits at the actor. From it a bolt runs to every target (references v1): a start point, the bolt's subdivisions, and an end point at the target.
     - Segments longer than +0x88 split at their middle, moved by up to ± jitter (+0x74) × length / 2.
     - A point stores how far along its bolt it is (1 − t, where t counts down from 1); its size falls with t when tapered (+0x98).
     - The end point clears particle flag 2, which ends the stream.
     - With follow (+0x9c, v2) each step moves the points by the first target's movement × that fraction.
   - **AutoExternalSpawner** (0x40001560). A new particle whose serial is a multiple of +0x6c, or one in +0x70 at random, has the external `ManualParticleSpawner` (references v1) spawn one at it: size × +0x74, life × +0x78.
   - **StreamParticleRenderer** (0x40017050, Process 0x400172b0). v1: [RGBA][texture mode +0x70][colour mode +0x74][width +0x6c][texture scale +0x78][BOOL breaks +0x80][texture offset +0x7c][gap +0x84].
     - Drawing: a strip through the live particles, two vertices per particle, half-width = size × width ÷ depth in screen space; each joint averages its segments.
     - Colour modes: 0/3 constant, 1 × age, 2 × (1 − age), 4 the value at age, 5 × the intensity channel.
     - Texture modes: 0 whole image, 2 a 4×4 frame by serial, 4 v = age × scale + scene time (ms) × +0x7c (corrected in 116).
     - It draws PowerPlant's and the pickups' lightning, fighter attack streamers, Pyros's fire stream and FirePit's steam.
   - **FourFaceTriangle** (0x40014320, Process 0x400145d0). v1: [RGBA][three corners][nine jitters][four floats, +0xc0 the texture scale][facing +200][texture plane +0xcc].
     - Each new particle gets its triangle: corners + up to their jitter, × size, turned by its orientation in mode 0. Its texture coordinates are the corners projected on XY / XZ / YZ × scale at a random offset.
     - It is drawn as three faces round a centre vertex at the particle. The centre takes the colour at age; the corners are black (additive) or transparent (alpha blend).
     - It draws BladePit's and Canyon's clouds and Iceberg's fog.
   - **Executors added as properties.** `CActor::AddProperty` of a `ClassExecutor` makes it one of the actor's properties. It runs when the actor is prepared into the scene; Jaguar's lasers get their `BLaser` behaviour this way.
     - Before this the laser had no behaviour at all.
     - `Scene().Add` attaches the branch's executors at once, but their entries run after the calling script continues. `BJaguar::Ground.FireLaser.Fire` calls `SetLength` and `SetAsProjectileCreator` after `Scene().Add`, and `BLaser::LeftStanding` reads both. Run at once, the laser cut itself short on Jaguar's own hit sphere and hit Jaguar.
   - **Static lights, per render affector.** `SetAffector` stores `IsPositionStatic` of the render affector itself (+0x10c), so a static light skips everything a static render affector covers. BladePit's moving fighting surfaces sit under the static arena-wide `LightingAffector`, so the arena's static spotlights leave them alone.
   - **Natives:**
     - `CConeAffector::Far` (+0x180);
     - `CActor::PointZ(CActor)` (Z towards the target's focal point);
     - `AddPotentialVictimFromHitStream` (the stream's hit count into +0xc0, then `AddPotentialVictim`);
     - the float `/=` operator.
115. **Point streamer trails and per-renderer visibility** [Core `DE_CPointStreamer` 0x701d1520, `DE_CInstanceInfo::Process` 0x701d17f0, `Enable`/`Disable` 0x701d2500; `CameraActorCaller` 0x70183530, `SetRendered`/`SetUnrenderedCaller` 0x7008e420]:
   - **Data.** `Mesh\PointStreamer` (after the mesh chunk). v2: [interval][life][start width][end width][start alpha][end alpha][texture length][int, cleared]; v3 adds [mode][rise]. Defaults: 50 ms, 2000 ms, 0.4, 2.5, 1, 0, 2000, mode 2, rise 0.006.
   - **Points.** While enabled, a point is added at the actor every interval, carrying its time and v = time ÷ texture length. Points older than the life are dropped, and each rises by rise × dt.
   - **Drawing.** A strip from the oldest point to the actor's current position:
     - half-width = start + (end − start) × age ÷ life, and alpha likewise;
     - the oldest point is transparent;
     - mode 0/1 writes grey × alpha, mode 2 white with alpha.
   - **Uses.** Fighter jet smoke, fireball trails, Pyros's exhaust smoke, Volcano's missile smoke and debris smoke.
   - **Per-renderer visibility.** `BRenderActorIfCamInTree` / `BRenderIfCamIsParent` show an actor only to renderers whose camera actor (`CRenderer::CameraActor`) sits in the right tree. There is one renderer here, whose camera is the focused fighter's `CameraActor`.
116. **Stream geometry, primitive meshes in world space, ProcessEvent** [Particles `DE_CStreamParticleRenderer::DE_CInstanceInfo::Process` 0x400172b0 (disassembled), Core `DE_CPointStreamer::DE_CInstanceInfo::Process` 0x701d17f0, `ProcessEventCaller` 0x70046230, `DE_CActor::ProcessEvent` 0x70090ed0, `DE_CObject::ProcessEvent` 0x7003b610, OMF `DE_COMF2Fighter::ProcessEvent` 0x35024d30, Core `DE_CPrimitiveGeneratorMesh::Process` 0x7012bde0, `CTextPrimitive::Setup` 0x7012df90]:
   - **Stream fields.** The first int is the texture mode (+0x70), the second the colour mode (+0x74): `+0x74 == 5` picks the intensity channel and the colour switch (0x40018117, cases 1-5) loads +0x74. Shipped pairs: texture 0/2/4, colour 2/3/4/5.
   - **Colour modes** (jump table 0x400185dc): 1 all four × age; 2 × (1 − age); 3 constant; 4 the colour value at age, rgb × alpha, alpha 1; 5 rgb × alpha × intensity, alpha 1; 0 a constant written once.
   - **Texture modes.** 0: each quad the whole image, written once. 2: frame = serial & 15, u = frame × 0.25 (wraps), v = (frame >> 2) × 0.25. 4: v = age × +0x78 + time × +0x7c, where the time is the one handed to `SetTime` (scene ms; the data's +0x7c are scroll rates like -0.003 and 0.0001). Modes 0-2 draw four vertices per particle (separate quads); the others share a strip unless breaks without a gap. The joints are averaged either way, so the geometry is the same.
   - **Stream geometry.** Each point is taken into camera space, depth clamped to at least 2, projected (x / z × focal + centre). A segment's half-width is size (its first particle) × width × focal / depth of its second. Its direction is the sum of its own and the previous segment's screen delta (+0.001 on the length). A joint averages (× 0.5) the two segments' vertices. There is no actor scale.
   - **Trail geometry.** Each point is offset perpendicular on screen to the way to the next point (the actor's to the way from the last), by its width / depth. Where either point is within depth 1 the point gets no width and alpha 0. The trails' materials are 8 / 4 / 0x40 for modes 0 / 1 / 2, the particle renderers' mapping (additive, additive, alpha).
   - **ProcessEvent.** `CObject::ProcessEvent(CEvent)` calls the object's ProcessEvent (vtable +0x70). An actor passes it to each property (the script event relays among them) and then to `DE_CObject::ProcessEvent`, which handles only the editor's drag-and-drop events. `DE_COMF2Fighter::ProcessEvent` forwards OMF2 types 1-10 to its recipient lists; the projectiles' arena surface hit (0xb) has no handler.
   - **Primitive meshes.** A `PrimitiveGenerator` mesh is an ordinary dynamic mesh: its primitives' vertices are in the mesh's frame, and `SetAsScreenSpace` (+0x5c) only switches the element to screen mode (3). `BText` sets screen space only when its `m_SetScreenSpace` member is set (no data default), so the crowd-response texts (`BCrowdResponseSlot`, which places them with screen positions) stay in world space.

117. **Frame timing, temporal sets, scene order, fighter assembly** [Core `DE_CScene::PassTime` 0x70070040, `TemporalElementSetForRate` 0x70072230, `AddTemporal` 0x70071e80, `DE_CTemporalElementSet::AdvanceTime` 0x70072090, `DE_CClassExecutor::ObjProcessContents` 0x701434b0, `ObjPrepare` 0x70143840, `DE_CClassExecutorInstance::Setup` 0x70144df0, `Tick` 0x70145700, `DE_CScene::Add` 0x7006ed50, `Remove` 0x7006ef00, `BehaviorInterfacePointerCaller` 0x70046310, `DE_CObject::ObjBrainInstance` 0x7003ddc0, `DE_CPassiveObjectReference::ObjPrepare` 0x7004db70, `DE_CDescription::NewResource` 0x701f2170, `ObjProcessReferences` 0x701f17a0, `DE_CMeshAffector::DE_CMeshReference::IsTouchingCone` 0x70085140 / `AsLineSegment` 0x700854e0, `DE_CSimplePhysics::ObjProcessReferences` 0x701db8b0, `DE_CCommonForceParticleSpawningMaterial::ProcessForce` 0x7020a160 (disassembled), `ApplyForceCollisionResultsCaller` 0x7007abb0; Creature `DE_CCreature::Tick` 0x15016870, `SetupControl` 0x15016fa0, `ObjNumBrainInstance` 0x15017470; OMF `DE_COMF2Fighter::Tick` 0x350253a0, `ObjPrepare`, `DE_CFighterPhysics::Tick` 0x35039970, `DE_CPilot::Attack` 0x3504d280; Session `DE_CStandardSessionSlot::OwnCreature` 0x4501aa60, `SetupCreatureControl` 0x4501a9b0, `DE_CStandardSession::ObjBrainInstance` 0x4500f610]:
   - **PassTime(ms).** The scene clock is real ms (scaled by +0x178, fractions carried). One temporal set per rate, made on first use with its last tick at (now / rate) × rate. Until the frame's time is used up, the set whose next tick (last + rate) comes first advances (the first made wins a tie): the clock stands at that tick, and `AdvanceTime` ticks its elements from the newest. The clock then moves on by what is left. After that, once per frame: the delta-tick script hooks (+200) with the frame's ms, then the delta-tick elements (+0x70). `DE_CWindow::Run` runs a frame once 20 ms have passed.
   - **Temporals.** Creatures with a SceneTicksPerUpdate; executors whose `ClassExecutor` has an interval (+0x44: v2 16, v3+ stored; 229 of 284 shipped have 0 and never tick, running only from hooks and events; the pilots' is 200, the music player's 100); collision testers (16, or their interval); common forces with a rate; physics not driven by a creature; the input manager (16); hit sparks and effects (the fighter's rate). `Setup` always passes the scene, so an executor with an interval is its own temporal even when it is also its creature's behaviour.
   - **Executor tick.** A pending wait (+0x104) counts down one per tick; otherwise `ContinueExecution`. So a script's waits count its own executor's ticks.
   - **Creature tick.** The command manager (+0x118), then the physics (+0x1cc), then the behaviour executor (+0x1a0, the first of `GatherBehaviorInstances`), then the state hooks, and the update count (+0x1d4) goes up. `DE_COMF2Fighter::Tick` adds `Guide`, the four attack-trailer points, and, for a fighter no controller drives that the local machine simulates (+0x1f8), the attack-range step every 64 updates: base (+0x5f4) += 0.065; r = Rand(2 − 1 / the pilot's last Attack aggression, +0x3dc); r > 0.9: × 0.6; r < 0.1: + 0.4; kept within 0.65 .. 1.3.
   - **Fighter physics** runs once prepared (+0x1a8) and enabled (+0x38); there is no wait for the spawn.
   - **Scene list.** `DE_CScene::Add` adds an actor's children first (the last first), then the actor itself to the affector list (+0x184, when +0x124 is 0 and +0x178 set). `Remove` takes it out keeping the others' order. `IsTouching` walks this list.
   - **Fighter links.** `DE_COMF2Fighter::ObjPrepare` links each fighter already in the scene (factory order) with the new one, both ways (+0x334). Pushes walk the list from the last; `NumEnemies`/`Enemy` and `CurrentTarget` use it.
   - **Interface().** It searches `ObjBrainInstance`: an object's own executors; a session's, then each modifier's (+0x88) in order; a slot's slot modifiers' (+0x54); a creature's, then its components' (+0x194) without flag 0x20. Children are not searched.
   - **Fighter assembly.** The robot's data carries its executors (`StandardJaguarExecutor` = `PJaguar` at `Entry`, `EPreCache`) and an `OMFFighterControllerReference`. A `PassiveObjectReference` v1 is [name][passive reference][BOOL +0x2c create when prepared][BOOL +0x30 under the reference's parent]: on prepare it copies its target, here `OMFFighterController` (a `JoystickInputManager` with the `StandardFighterHookupExecutor`), under the fighter. `BFighter` finds both by name. `OwnCreature` renames the creature and adds it to the scene, then `SetupCreatureControl` binds the user's client (controller +0x144, camera +0x148), sets +0x1f8 and `SetupControl` gathers the `CreatureController` properties.
   - **Descriptions.** v6 references are [member casts][BOOL icon][BOOL large][resource components][replacement objects][BOOL medium]. `NewResource` copies the resource (+0x98), replaces each descendant named like a replacement object with a copy of it, and adds a copy of each component. Shipped: no replacements; components are the session modifiers' values (ScoreLimit, SessionSpeed) and some environments' extra actors (Laser Ball, PracticeArena).
   - **Cones.** With a penumbra, `IsTouchingCone` on a mesh classifies the vertices, then runs an empty loop over the faces and returns 0; on a box it returns 0. With penumbra 0 both test as a line segment: each face the segment crosses (from its back to its front) is a collision, unless the results ask for the first only (+0x18) or the nearest (+0x3c, with +0x38 keeping only it). Scripts set neither, so every crossed face counts.
   - **Simple physics force.** v2 references are [test affectors][BOOL + force (+0x60)]. On prepare the force's actor is set to the physics' actor (or that affector's owner); after a bounce, the force is applied to the collision results.
   - **Particle-spawning material actor.** The created actor stands at the touch point with Z along the surface normal. With +0xc4 (the BOOL after the mask), on a mesh face the face's vertices are taken in that frame. The lowest and highest X, minus the touch point's, become `BeginX`/`EndX` on the created actor and on the force, which `BLightningWall::ProcessForce` reads. Used by City's and SpaceStation's force fields and PowerPlant's wall trigger.
   - **ApplyForce on results.** `CCollisionResults::ApplyForce(force, i)` sends collision i's touched affector a collision event carrying the force (every collision with i = −1). Jaguar's laser and the standard projectile use it.
   - **Surface change.** `AddSurfaceChangeRecipient` adds to the fighter's type-3 list (+0x348). The type-3 event (+0x354) is made at init but never sent, so those hooks never run.
118. **Engine leftovers** [Core `DE_CScene::Add` 0x7006ed50, `DE_CActor::ObjSetParent` 0x7008afb0, `DE_CMaterial::ProcessForce` 0x7020bb30, `DE_CPCExecuter::ContinueExecutionInternal` 0x7014eaa0 (case 0x22), `DE_CClass::LoadVMCode` 0x70134530; Creature `DE_CCreature::SetAsUserControlled` 0x15016ca0 (vtable +0x144), `Camera` (+0x148), `DE_CLookAtCommand::Update` 0x15020ea0; OMF `AddTouchFloorRecipientCaller`, `DE_CPCodeEventRelay::ProcessEvent` 0x3502eea0, `DE_CPilot::ObjProcessContents` 0x3504b450; Session `DE_CStandardSessionSlotModifier::AttachComponents`; scripts `BCreatureAndPilotPicker`]:
   - **Preparation order.** `Add` prepares the actor (`ObjPrepare`, vtable +0xcc: its temporals and executors), then adds its children, the last first, then appends it to the affector list. Actors that `ShouldBeInnactive` are skipped.
   - **Parenting.** `ObjSetParent` under an actor of another scene (or of none) takes the actor out of its old scene and calls the new one's `Add`, so the branch is prepared and joins the affector list there and then. A move within a scene changes nothing.
   - **Control.** `SetupCreatureControl` calls `SetAsUserControlled(device)`: +0x1fc = device, +0x1f8 = 1, each controller bound (vtable +0x168), the behaviour executor and state manager told. `Camera(0)` gives the scene viewer's camera.
   - **Floor events.** The touch/leave-floor recipients are `DE_CPCodeEventRelay`s; their `ProcessEvent` calls the method with a zero argument, so the handlers get no event.
   - **Material rate limit.** A material with a threshold has its own `CIncidentRecorder` (+0x68). Each touch calls `AddIncidentIfAbsent(time, owner)` (the touched affector's +0x118, else the affector), and an incident of that owner within the threshold, inclusive, blocks it.
   - **Look-at offset.** `DE_CLookAtCommand::Update` aims at the target's position + the offset (+0x7c). `SetOffset` has no caller, so the offset stays zero.
   - **Slot components.** `AttachComponents` copies each of the slot modifier's components and hands it to the creature's `AddComponent` (vtable +0x188); there is no check for one already there.
   - **Pilots.** `AttemptToCreatePilot` makes the pilot from its description (`NewResource`) and parents the description under it. `AttemptToCreateCreature` parents the pilot under the creature, which the slot then owns.
   - **Op 0x22.** A call to a state from an instant method destroys the instance's main executer frames and `SetState`s it with the arguments, ending the instant call. A call to a state from a state sets it and continues there. An instant method called from a state runs on the instance's second executer (+0x8c), and if the state changed during it (+0xf0), the caller is abandoned.
   - **VMC2 end.** `LoadVMCode` seeks to the chunk end once the final instances are read, so the bytes after them are never interpreted.
119. **Scene membership** [Core `DE_CActor::ObjSetParent` 0x7008afb0, `DE_CActor::SetActive`, `DE_CActor::SetInnactive`, `DE_CActor::ShouldBeInnactive`, `DE_CScene::Add` 0x7006ed50]:
   - **Parking.** A child actor that is inactive (+0x110 bit 8 clear) or not shown at the current detail level (`ShouldBeInnactive`, detail flags 2/4) is kept in its parent's +0xec list, out of the scene: it is not prepared, ticked, drawn or collided with, and neither is its branch. A root added to the scene is in it whatever its own flag.
   - **Activation.** `SetActive` (vtable +0x124) moves the actor back among its parent's children and calls `Scene().Add`, preparing the branch then; `SetInnactive` parks it and calls `Scene().Remove`.
   - **Shipped data.** A City fight has 14 data actors that start inactive, all templates (`LaserProjectile`, `GetHitAffector`, `SparkEffect`, ...) copied by scripts and activated on the copy.
120. **Music playback** [AudioPlayer `DE_CDirectXSpeakerElement::Play` 0x10005010, `Activate` 0x10004460, `Update` 0x100047b0, `CreateNewAudiopath`, `DE_CDirectXAudioPlayer::ProcessPerformanceMessages`; reference: WDK DDKSynth sample (`reference/ddksynth`, MIT), Wine `dmusici.h`]:
   - **Flags.** A speaker's segment flags (+0xa0, 1 from the constructor) gain `DMUS_SEGF_SECONDARY` (0x80) or, when its audiopath is a DirectMusic one (+0x74, set by `CreateNewAudiopath` for segments), `DMUS_SEGF_SEGMENTEND` (0x800000). `Activate` plays the segment with them plus `DMUS_SEGF_TIMESIG_ALWAYS` at time 0. Music segments therefore play as primary segments, each starting at the end of the one playing (at once when none is).
   - **Chaining.** `ProcessPerformanceMessages` takes `GUID_NOTIFICATION_SEGMENT` type 2 (`SEGALMOSTEND`) for the speaker element whose segment state (+0x7c/+0x80) sent it, picks the segment's noise for the speaker's mood (+0x58, 1..20: contents +0x7c + 12 (mood − 1)) and plays it. No `SetPrepareTime` call exists, so the notification comes at DirectMusic's default 1000 ms before the end.
   - **Level.** `Update` sets a segment speaker's audiopath volume (`IDirectMusicAudioPath::SetVolume`, vtable +0x14) like a wave speaker's buffer volume (FINDINGS 121): 10000 log10(1 − (1 − v s)²) in 1/100 dB. For music, v is the global music volume (registry `MusicVolume`, default 0.6; only the options menu's slider sets it) times s, the speaker's volume (1), so the music plays at −7.6 dB. When the application loses focus, `PlayAudio` sets `GUID_PerfMasterVolume` to −10000 (and back to 0).
   - **Synth.** `InitAudio(.., DMUS_APATH_DYNAMIC_STEREO, 128 pchannels, DMUS_AUDIOF_3D | BUFFERS)` uses the default software synth: 22050 Hz, 64 voices. Its behaviour is the DDKSynth sample's: volume in 1/100 dB, pitch in cents; envelope level 0..1000 as dB = 0.096 level − 96, attack through a log table, decay scaled by (1000 − sustain)/1000, release by the current level, quick stop at rate/70; control points at ¼, ½ and the end of each segment, at most 50 ms; linear interpolation, 16-bit voices >> 13 (8-bit >> 5), the sum doubled and clamped; velocity, CC7 and CC11 as 40 log10(x/127), pan as 10 log10; CC7 100, CC11 127, pan 64 and a ±200 cent bend by default; 64 + 8 voices, allocated by priority (0x80000000 + (16 − part), MIDI channel 10 being part 0) and stolen from the lowest; the first region whose key range holds the note; one extra sample per wave (0, or the loop start). `Open` resets the gain adjust to 0.
   - **Wave pool.** A region's link index selects a pool-table cue, whose value is the wave's offset in the wave pool, not its index.
121. **Sound speakers** [AudioPlayer `DE_CDirectXSpeakerElement::Update` 0x100047b0, `Play` 0x10005010, `Activate` 0x10004460, `SetActiveNoise` 0x10004370, `CreateNewAudiopath`, `DE_CDirectXListenerElement::Update` 0x10005490, `DE_CDirectXAudioPlayerFactory::ProcessEvent` (DirectSound setup); Core `DE_CScene::PlayNoiseSphere` 0x70072b70, `PlayNoiseAmbient` 0x70072c20, `DE_CSpeaker::NewNoisePlayerSphere` 0x70219e70, `DE_CSpeakerElement::GetNoiseVolume` 0x70215770, `DE_CSphereAffector::IsTouching` 0x7008a1b0; constants at AudioPlayer 0x1000fac4..0x1000fae8]:
   - **Speakers.** `PlayNoiseSphere` makes a sphere affector at the point carrying a speaker with the noise; `PlayNoiseAmbient` does the same at the origin with a 1e7 hotspot and falloff. The speaker element's volume comes from the sphere (`IsTouching`, vtable +0x164): 1 within the hotspot, 1 − (d − hotspot)/(falloff − hotspot) out to the falloff, else 0. That intensity is multiplied by the global SFX volume (registry `SFXVolume`, default 1.0) and the speaker's volume (+0x50), and capped at 1.
   - **Updates.** Every frame `Update` adds falloff/(distance + 1) × rate × frame ms to +0xac (10000 with no falloff) and does nothing more until it passes 200; then it zeroes it. The rate (+0xb0) is min(1, length/7000 + 0.1), from the noise's length (`SetActiveNoise`, vtable +0x118; taken as milliseconds), for non-looping noises (else 1). +0xac starts at 99999, so the first update runs.
   - **Volume.** The buffer's volume is 10000 log10(1 − (1 − v s)²), in 1/100 dB (`IDirectSoundBuffer::SetVolume`), v being the element's volume and s the speaker's volume again; for v = 0 the value is +10000, out of range, so the call fails and the old volume stays.
   - **Starting.** `Play` does not start a speaker at or beyond its falloff (nor one of no volume). Looping and segment speakers (flag 0x4000000) start once their volume passes 0.09 and stop below 0.03; one-shots are never stopped by volume.
   - **Position.** With hardware 3D buffers, the buffer's minimum and maximum distance are set to 9999999 (`SetMinDistance`/`SetMaxDistance`) for sphere and cone speakers, so DirectSound3D only adds direction. In head-relative mode (speaker +0x98) `Update` puts (speaker − listener) in the listener's frame and calls `SetPosition(−x, y, z)`, DirectSound being left-handed. At full volume a sound behind the listener is mirrored to the front, and one inside its hotspot (for a speaker not on an affector, whose `GetNoiseVolume` gives d/hotspot) is drawn toward the centre: x × (d/hotspot)², z + (1 − d/hotspot) × 200. The listener is positioned and oriented every frame (`SetPosition`, `SetOrientation`, deferred, committed in `PlayAudio`).
   - **Hardware 3D only.** The 3D flag (factory +0x184) is set only when `IDirectSound::GetCaps` reports free hardware 3D buffers (`DSCAPS.dwFreeHw3DAllBuffers`, +0x15c). Without them no `IDirectSound3DBuffer` exists and sounds play centred, with no pan at all. The DirectSound3D listener itself comes from the primary buffer through the default audiopath.
   - **Not used.** Nothing calls `SetVelocity` on a buffer or the listener, `SetDopplerFactor` or `SetRolloffFactor`, so there is no Doppler effect (zero velocity), and there is no occlusion or obstruction. Each sound gets its own duplicated buffer; AudioPlayer has no voice limit of its own.
122. **Music against the soundtrack** [the MP3s at omf.niraj.ws/bgmedia.htm, kept in `reference/bgmedia` and not redistributed; our renders recorded with `OPENBG_RECORD_AUDIO`, 110 s each]:
   - **Songs.** Arenas play: City and Canyon Where the Mind Is (`MusicWTMI`), Desert Snakeirons, BladePit K-Faktor, FirePit and Volcano Hexed, Iceberg Crystal Cold, PowerPlant and SpaceStation Executor. `MusicSongNine` has no MP3.
   - **Matches.** Tempo is the same (Crystal Cold 129.2 BPM, Where the Mind Is 161.5 BPM), and the tuning of the strong spectral peaks is within 5 cents of the MP3s'.
   - **Treble.** Ours has 6 dB less energy at 1-4 kHz, 12 dB less at 4-11 kHz and nothing above 11 kHz. Running the synth at 44100 Hz changes this by about 1 dB, because the songs' DLS waves are mostly 11025 Hz (10) and 22050 Hz (27), with only 13 at 44100 Hz. The MP3s are studio renders from fuller sources.
   - **Silent parts.** Crystal Cold's band gives performance channels 7 and 8 no patch and no DLS reference (flags GM | GS only), and no band in any song uses the GM set. In Microsoft's synth those channels stay on program 0, which no downloaded instrument has, so their notes (about 470 each in two minutes) are silent, here as in the game. K-Faktor and Hexed have a few such notes too.
123. **OMFBG.exe and its "natives"** [`source/omfbg-exe/REPORT.md` and `data/` (the exe's COFF symbol table and `.rdata` import names, both readable; `.text`/`.data` stay encrypted and are not decrypted); Core `DE_CObject` registrations around 0x70045a20, `DE_GLOBAL::AddObjectToClean` 0x70011c80, `HouskeepingObjects` 0x70011b00, `UpdateSystem` 0x700117f0]:
   - **The exe is the launcher.** Its 945 functions are the front end (`DE_CGameLauncherWindow`, `DE_CGUIH`, `DE_CControl3d`, `DE_CGUIStandardInterface`) and the session glue (`GEH_GUI_*`, `_GH_SESSION_*`, `_GH_SLOT_*` event handles registered with `RegisterGenericEventHandle`). Its engine imports include no `DE_CClass` and no native registration, so it registers no natives.
   - **`Die` is Core's.** `DE_CObject`'s method block names `DeleteThis`, `Die`, `DeleteThisLater`; the registrations bind them to `DeleteThisCaller`, `DeleteThisLaterCaller`, `DeleteThisLaterCaller`. So `Die` is `DeleteThisLater`: `AddObjectToClean` queues the object, and `HouskeepingObjects` destroys the queue (last first) at the start of `UpdateSystem`'s update and again after the update objects and auto-updates. An object that dies plays out the rest of its frame.
   - **Never called.** `CTextPrimitive::SetShadowOff`, `COMF2Fighter::IsEvadeButtonDown`, `CSimplePhysics::SetGravity` and `CCollisionEvent::SourceAffector` are in no module and in none of the 971 intrinsics the shipped bytecode calls (`re/intrinsics-used.txt`); the text intrinsic is `SetShadowOn`, the duck button `IsDuckButtonDown`, and `SetGravity` in the data is the scripts' own `LaunchableSetGravity`. Our registrations of them were removed. (FINDINGS 102's "eleven native keys the scripts use" counted our registrations, not the scripts' calls.)
124. **The launcher (OMFBG.exe)** [`source/omfbg-exe/REPORT.md` §5e: a plaintext 2004-03 build of the launcher (build 2172, later than the retail 2003-11 exe, whose code stays encrypted), decompiled from its intact COFF symbols; behaviour only, nothing of its code is used. `_WinMain@16`, `DE_CGameLauncherWindow::ObjInitContents` and `ProcessEvent`]:
   - **Startup.** WinMain sets the window instance, creates Direct3D 9 (or shows an error and quits), sets the viewport GUID, calls `DE_GLOBAL::SystemInit`, installs a `DE_CGameLauncherInterface` (a `DE_CGUIStandardInterface`) as the GUI interface, creates the `GameLauncherWindow` and shows it (vtable +0x218 with 1), reports the system started and runs the message loop until shutdown.
   - **Window setup.** `ObjInitContents` registers the window as an update object and as `DE_CGUIH::MainWindow` (with `ID_DEBUG_GUI` turning on the debug GUI), runs `DE_CGUIH::Init`, asks for the splash image with a `GH_GET_SPLASH_SCREEN_OBJECT` generic event and stamps "[Build: 2172]" text over it, then builds a child control holding a `DE_CSceneViewerControl3d` whose new scene gets a temporal master and its scene watchers. It runs `InitGUI` and, unless a command-line switch is given (the report names `connect`), creates the `MainMenuUIManager` and makes it the app manager's next UI manager. A dedicated server shows a red status text instead.
   - **Preferences.** It reads only `Network\Server Name` and `Network\Player Name`; each sets a flag when it is non-empty. Difficulty, shadows and the volumes are not the launcher's (OMF/Core).
   - **First run.** On control event 0x3a, if `DE_GLOBAL::FirstRun` is set (Core sets it when the registry file did not exist), there is no `simulatefullscreen` switch and training hasn't been started, it shows a "Welcome" notification and launches `SinglePlayer\Basic Training` (`DE_CSessionMasterDescription::LaunchSession`). Control event 0x3a otherwise starts the UI once (`DE_CAppManager::BeginUI`) and checks exclusive mode; control event 7 makes a fresh scene in the viewer and shows it.
   - **Session display.** `GH_GET_SCENE_VIEWER` from a session that `IsDisplayNeeded` switches the viewer to exclusive mode on that scene and hands the viewer back in the event. On attaching a scene to the viewer it creates the scene's audio player (`DE_CAudioPlayer::CreateAudioPlayer`) and makes it the master player.
   - **Not the exe's.** No native registration exists in the launcher (its only registration call is `RegisterGenericEventHandle`, for its named handles).
125. **UI managers and page flow** [Core `DE_CAppManager::SetNextUIManager` 0x7019af60, `UpdateUI` 0x7019b360, `TransitionUIManager` 0x7019ab30, `PageTransitionBusy` 0x7019b8f0, `SignalStartPageTransitionBegin`/`End` 0x7019b750/0x7019b820, `BeginUI` 0x7019b9f0, `DeleteUIManagerStack` 0x7019ba00, `CloseUIManagerStack` 0x7019bd00, `SetLastUIManager` 0x7019b100, `IsUIManagerHaltingLoading`/`Autopaused`/`DisableUserCreatureInput` 0x7019b200..0x7019b320; `DE_CUIManager::ObjInitContents` 0x701ea9a0, `Begin` 0x701eb440, `Activate` 0x701eb8a0, `Deactivate` 0x701eb6e0, `UIManagerComplete` 0x701eb320, `SetupFolderControls` 0x701ebc70, `PositionControl` 0x701ebdf0, `SetupDisplayArea` 0x701ec3f0, `SetupControlArea` 0x701ec270, `GetObjectByValueString` 0x701ecbb0; OMF `DE_COMF2Manager` vftable 0x35078d50, `MenuSelect` 0x350151d0, `ProcessEvent` 0x350155c0, `ObjUpdate` 0x350150f0, the OMF managers' `ObjInitContents`/`SetupControls`/`ProcessButtonClickEvent`]:
   - **State.** Three static slots, current (0x7035e0dc), next (0x7035e0bc) and last (0x7035e0cc), plus a stack on the app manager (+0x3c/+0x40). App manager +0x74 is the transition state, +0x78 "the current page is finished" (set by any manager's Complete event and by `SetNextUIManager(NULL)`). A manager's +0x9c is "begun", +0xa0 "activated"; flags default autodestroy +0xa4, transition +0xa8, main frame +0xac, backdrop +0xb0, autopause +0xb4, input off +0xbc, overlay +0xc8 to 1 and halt loading +0xb8 to 0; +0xcc is its GUI folder name ("Default").
   - **SetNextUIManager(mgr, target, push).** Clears +0x78. Auto-select mode (+0x7c or the `auto_select` cookie) sets mgr+0x94. The very first call makes mgr next and runs its `Begin` at once. Otherwise: no-op when mgr is current or next; last = current, pushed when `push` and not on the stack; mgr NULL sets +0x78; the old next is released; next = mgr, its notification target (+0x30) the given one or the app manager.
   - **UpdateUI** (every app update, before the sessions): activates the transition actor while a page change is wanted and drives exclusive (game-only) mode when no UI is up; shows the standard session overlay while a page is current over a rendering session scene, unless the page clears +0xc8; runs `TransitionUIManager` when one is due; activates a begun, unfinished current page; pops the stack when nothing is current or next; and `Begin`s a next page that has not begun (a 0 return vetoes or redirects it).
   - **TransitionUIManager.** State 0→1: send `GUIStartPageChangeTransition` to the transition actor (when the outgoing or incoming page has +0xa8). State 1, once not busy and the next page has begun (with no next page: once the last local session `IsFullySequenced`): deactivate the current page, delete it unless persistent, autodestroy-off or stacked, make next current and activate it, show/hide the main frame by +0xac and the backdrop by +0xb0 (never over a rendering session scene), send `GUIPageChangeTransitionCompleted`, state 2. State 2, once not busy: state 0, deactivate the transition actor. Busy = `GUIPageChangeTransitionBusy` sent with INT1 0 comes back non-zero. Stack: Back = `SetLastUIManager` (pop the top into next); `DeleteUIManagerStack(n)` deletes deletable entries and empties it (n: also close the current page); `CloseUIManagerStack` pops everything then closes.
   - **A page.** `Begin` sends the target an Activate event, deletes last time's created objects, runs Activate hooks and sets begun. `Activate` makes the page's dialog and a root actor under the UI parent, constructs `<transfer agent>\F.<folder>` (a `Value\String` there is followed once), collects the folder's actor children whose upper-cased names start `VE.`, re-parents them under the root, calls `SetupControls` and gives every `P.Description` actor a description control. `Deactivate` deletes the dialog and root actor (and the VE actors with it). `UIManagerComplete` clears begun and sends the Complete event to the target and the app manager.
   - **Markers.** `PositionControl(name)` finds `VE.<name>` (the prefix added if missing) case-insensitively among the VE actors. The rectangle comes from the bounding box of the first mesh of the VE actor's child `DisplayArea` (or the child its `P.DisplayArea` string names): with p = actor position + box min in the UI parent's frame and s the box size, left = p.x, right = p.x + s.x, top = 1 − (p.y + s.y)·4/3, bottom = 1 − p.y·4/3, as fractions of the parent control. A `P.ControlName` string renames the control.
   - **App manager.** `MenuSelect` is vtable +0x130 (Core's is empty). 2/3/4/5 open a `GenericDescriptionSelector` named "ChooseSesssionType" (sic), title "Choose Match", autoselect key "Session Master", over the master `SessionMasterDescription`s: 3 those whose cast list holds `COREMeshes\SinglePlayerCast` (value `SinglePlayer`), 2 `MultiPlayerCast` (value `Joinable`), each kept only when visible-when-locked or not locked; 4 and 5 all of them (4 also turns on auto-select). 7/8 add an AI bot (server only), 10 opens the session browser. When the chooser completes (`ProcessEvent`): a standard session info from the chosen description (`SinglePlayer` → info+0xdc and content-controllable; `Joinable` → info+0xbc), the session made and set as server, `SatisfyMasterRequirements`; for a top-level session without clients a local user-controlled client named "User", registry `SinglePlayerPlayed`, `AddIfAbsentLocalSession` and the session's `Begin`. `ObjUpdate`: with no local session and no UI showing, a MainMenuUIManager becomes next (the way back after a match). `Activate` reads registry `AI Difficulty` into +0xd8.
   - **Screens.** MainMenu (folder MainMenu, no main frame): Options, Replays, Single Player, Join Game, Host Game, Help, Quit at markers OPTIONS, REPLAYS, SINGLEPLAYER, MULTIPLAYERJOIN, MULTIPLAYERCREATE, HELP, QUIT; each click first empties the stack; Single Player = `MenuSelect(3)` and remembers "Singleplayer", Host = `MenuSelect(2)`, Join = `MenuSelect(10)` after up to three nag notices while `SinglePlayerPlayed` is unset; the others open their manager pushed. `Begin` empties the stack, plays the default music and honours `menu_*` cookies; in auto-select mode it reopens the remembered choice. Quit (Yes/No: quit or back). Credits (Back; opened by nothing in the modules). Help (a description selector over `COREMeshes\HelpEntry` descriptions, folder ChooserListBack, no Next). Options (folder Options, halts loading: Controls, Video, Audio, Game, Network panels in WINDOW, Back/Esc). In-game menu (Esc impulse "In Game Menu"; folder InGameMenuServer / InGameMenuSinglePlayer / InGameMenuClient / InGameMenuDedicatedServer by session kind; no transition, no main frame; Main Menu destroys the local sessions, Resume closes the stack).
   - **Not in the modules.** Nothing readable calls the app manager's setters (transfer agent, UI parent actor/control, scene, main window, main frame, transition, backdrop, session overlay); the launcher's `InitGUI` is the only candidate and its code is not readable. *Inference* from the data (FINDINGS 126): transfer agent `OMFBGGUI`, main frame `V.MainFrame`, transition `V.MainDoors`, backdrop `V.Backdrop`, overlay `V.StandardSceneOverlay`.
126. **Controls and the menu scene** [Core `DE_CControl3d` (`Scale*` 0x700e4e50..0x700e4ff0, `OffsetFrom*`, `CreateVisualControl` 0x700e3e80), `DE_CTopControl3d::ProcessEvent` 0x70102730, `AdjustRootActor` 0x70104420, `AssureRenderObjects` 0x70103870, `DE_CButtonControl3d::CreateVisualControl` 0x700bde40 / `SetText` 0x700bdb20 / `Click` 0x700bdd60, `DE_CButtonRobot::ProcessMouseEvent` 0x700c4fd0, `DE_CGUIStandardInterface::CreateControl` 0x700f4b90, `gNewText` 0x700b7870, `DE_CUIManagerActorButtonRobot` 0x701ed410..0x701ed940, `DE_CUIManagerRobot` 0x701ed050, `NewButtonControl` 0x701ece40, `DE_CSceneViewerControl3d::Camera` 0x700c1e70, `DE_CNotification::NewUserInformation` 0x70180550, `DE_CWindow::ConvertToVPU` 0x700d4fa0; OMF `gNewOutlinePanel` 0x35002a80, `DE_CUISizes` 0x35006f50..; scripts GUIEventFramework, BCommonButton, BButton, BDoorMover, BStandardSceneOverlay in OMFBGGUI]:
   - **Controls are flat meshes.** Each control owns a `DE_CControlActor3d` in the top control's own scene ("FrameControl::Scene", ambient 0.2, a 60° camera); the root actor sits one unit in front of it, scaled so its local [0,1] spans the viewport width and [0, H/W] its height, origin bottom-left. Edges are fractions of the parent (`Scale*`, +0xc4) plus pixel insets and lengths (+0xb8) in virtual 800×600 pixels. A control draws nothing unless its frame bit is set; text is a `DE_CFlatText3` mesh (the same fonts as the HUD). A menu button is an undecorated `DE_CButtonControl3d` (type 0): an invisible hit rectangle with a 16-pixel label in the interface's `ColorButtonText`, alignment 2. OMF panels are `gNewOutlinePanel`: a filled back panel, a 2-pixel outline inset 4 at alpha 0.9, an inner panel inset 2 more (alpha 0.3 unselected, 0.8 selected).
   - **Input.** The top control tracks the control under the mouse (control events 0x25 enter, 0x26 leave), gives presses to the hit control and focus, releases to the focused one; a button clicks (0x33, queued) when released over itself; Enter/Esc keys click bound buttons (`DE_CButtonClickOnKey`). A page's `DE_CUIManagerRobot` hears every button's click (`NewButtonControl` subscribes it) and the screen's `ProcessButtonClickEvent` compares the event's control (+0x54) with its buttons: navigation is C++.
   - **The 3D buttons.** `SetupDisplayArea` gives each positioned control a `DE_CUIManagerActorButtonRobot` that sends the VE actor the generic events `GUIEnter` (mouse enter, unless disabled), `GUILeave`, `GUISelect` (click), `GUILeave` once at setup and after the first draw, and passes `GUISetDescription` to the page; after the first draw it keeps the control glued to the DisplayArea actor's movement. The scripts only animate: BCommonButton spins `ButtonRotator`/`ButtonRotator2` (`RotateZSpeed` ±0.5, 0 on leave), turns `Default/ButtonSpark01..04` and `Default/Click` particles on and off and plays `Noises/MouseEnter` / `ButtonClick`; BButton~2/~4 (Back/Next) light their arc particles. GUIEventFramework's timer returns at once and its `Setup()` call is commented out, so no script navigates. BDoorMover on `V.MainDoors` slides Door01/Door02 in on `GUIStartPageChangeTransition` (350 ms), out on `GUIPageChangeTransitionCompleted` (500 ms, distance 0.52, 55 ms delay, `DoorSound`) and answers Busy while moving. BStandardSceneOverlay draws a full-screen modulated quad (0.4, 0.55, 0.7) of the background image.
   - **Event names** (Core registrations): `GUISetDescription`, `GUIAutoSetDescription`, `GUIEnter`, `GUILeave`, `GUISelect`, `GUISetup`, `GUIQuitGame`, `GUIPageChangeTransitionBusy`, `GUIStartPageChangeTransition`, `GUIPageChangeTransitionCompleted`. The launcher's `GEH_GUI_*` handles carry the GUIEvents script's other names (GUIGoTo, GUIBack, GUIDone, GUIResolveList, ...), which no shipped script reaches.
   - **The menu scene.** OMFBGGUI lays every screen out in 0..1 × 0..0.75 world units at z ≈ 0 (the `DisplayArea` boxes, `V.MainFrame`, the logo, the doors), with `V.*` actors persistent (MainFrame, MainDoors, Backdrop, Environment, StandardSceneOverlay) and one `F.<Screen>` folder per page. *Inference:* the launcher's UI parent actor is the top control's root actor, so the GUI scene is seen exactly as the controls are (the 4/3 in `SetupControlArea` is that frame's W/H), through a 60° horizontal field of view; no camera object exists in the data, and a scene viewer's default camera (0, 0, −100) would not frame it.
   - **Notifications.** `NewUserInformation(title, text)` shows a modal dialog (title, wrapped text, OK; Enter/Esc/click close) or queues it until dialogs are possible.
   - **Natives the GUI scripts use that fights did not:** `CGenericEvent::Text`/`SetText`/`Add`, and the primitive generator's `SetTexture`, `SetBlendModulate`, `SetSortValue`, `UVAsBox`, `PositionAsBox`, `SetColor` (the overlay quad).
127. **Single player** [OMF `DE_COMF2Manager::MenuSelect`/`ProcessEvent` (125); Core `DE_CGenericDescriptionSelector` (`ObjInitContents` 0x701e46c0, `GetAvailableDescriptions` 0x701e4790, `SetupControls` 0x701e4a60, `Begin` 0x701e4d40, robot 0x701e5130/0x701e5370), `IsMemberOfCaller` 0x701f1130, `DE_CSessionMasterDescription::Update` 0x701ff4c0, `IsLocked` 0x701f2640, `IsVisibleWhenLocked` 0x701f2220, `EndAndDieCaller` 0x701f7d90, `DE_CSession::End` 0x701f9400, `SubSessionOwnerCaller` 0x701f7dd0; OMF `DE_CChooseFighterUIManager` 0x35056420..0x350574b0, `DE_CChooseFighterModelPickerUIManager` 0x35053fb0..0x35055a10, `DE_CRulesDisplay` 0x35062ce0..0x350643d0; Session `GameDialogCaller` 0x4500e6d0; scripts BSinglePlayerSession, BMaster*, BSubSession*, BSinglePlayerSubSession, BCreatureAndPilotPicker, BRulesDisplay]:
   - **Flow.** Single Player → "Choose Match" (the single-player session masters: Training, Practice, North American Open, Redemption, HAR Master, Ultimate Warrior, IFL Cup, World Open, and SinglePlayerFull's and Trophy Room's) → the master session (`BSinglePlayerSession` + the tournament's `BMaster`) with slot 0's `BCreatureAndPilotPicker` → Choose Team (team games), Choose Pilot, Choose Creature → each match a sub-session of the tournament's game type, its picks copied in (`SubSessionPick*ByGUID`, so its choosers are skipped), behind the RulesDisplay screen → loss replays the match, win advances, `matchCinematic` runs the cinematic → a key press deletes the master session → main menu (125, `ObjUpdate`), whose auto-select reopens Single Player.
   - **Description selector.** Folder ChooserListNextBack (ListWindow, Window, NEXT, BACK); a name-sorted list of the pool's descriptions that are listed (+0xbc, default 1) and not hidden by a lock; the highlighted one's large representation (its `ControlCreator`) in Window; Next (Enter, double-click) chooses (completes) unless locked ("Selection Locked"), Back (Esc) backs out. `Begin`: a one-entry pool is chosen without showing; backing out passes through such a screen; auto-select picks by key. `ChosenDescription()` is +0x128.
   - **Choose fighter.** The selector plus a Model combo (the fighter's `FighterModelDescription`s, the flagged one by default) and a View button: the model picker (folder LoadFrame, marker LoadFrame) shows `LoadScene\ChooseFighterModelPickerEnvironment` through `EnvironmentCamera`, a copy of `LoadScene\FighterTemplate` with the last pilot's paint and the stripped model on `RobotPedistal`; arrows step the model. `ChosenModel()` is that model's GUID.
   - **RulesDisplay.** Folder SinglePlayerLoading (MatchScreenShot when a snapshot exists, CommentaryText, NextTextBox, MatchName, Progress; any other folder only InfoWindow). Under a sub-session owner it shows the owner's `MatchCommentary`, `NextMatchDescription`, `NextMatchName` (falling back to its master description's values and name) and `SnapshotImage`; with the single-player flag (info+0xdc) it is not authorized until a key is pressed after "Press any key to continue" appears; Progress shows `SetCurrentTask`'s text before that.
   - **Engine semantics.** `IsMemberOf(cast)`: the cast is in the description's cast list (+0x34), which for a session master description also holds its master modifier's casts (`Update`). `EndAndDie`: `DeleteThisLater` and `End` (stop the recorder, destroy clients, delete modifiers; the standard session also its slots); no UI. `SubSessionOwner`: the creating session (+0xcc), null at top level. `GameDialog` returns null.
   - **First run.** The 2004 launcher launches `SinglePlayer\Basic Training`; retail data has no such object (its first tournament is `Training`) and retail Core has no `LaunchSession`.
